## INTRODUCTION - ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT  [939010957]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > INTRODUCTION - ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939010957

#### WARNINGS
> Note: This vehicle is equipped with a starting system known as Smart-Key, whereby the traditional ignition key has been replaced with an electronic key "T.E.G.", (Transmitter for Entry and Go), combined with an electronic key reader and TEG-Reader radio frequency receiver.
Access to the vehicle through radiofrequency recognition of the remote control incorporated into the TEG is entirely the same as what takes place with the traditional remote control incorporated into the key fob.
The vehicle is not switched on/started up by inserting the key in the ignition switch (which is no longer present) and turning it, but rather by inserting the TEG in the TEG Reader and activating the START/STOP button.
The distribution of the power supply to the vehicle (ignition-controlled INT, INT/A, AVV, etc.) is managed by the steering lock node and takes place in entirely the same way as for models equipped with a traditional key.
The following documentation often still uses the expressions Key ON, Key OFF, ignition-operated power supply (INT) or key to MAR, key to STOP, key to AVV. These must be interpreted in accordance with the following summary table:
| Key ON | TEG inserted in TEG Reader |
| --- | --- |
| Key OFF | TEG removed from TEG Reader |
| ignition controlled power supply (INT) | TEG inserted into TEG Reader |
| key on MAR | TEG inserted in TEG Reader |
| key on STOP | TEG removed from TEG Reader |
| key on AVV | TEG inserted and START/STOP button pressed |

#### PRECAUTIONS
Before working on the electrical system (connectors, electrical components, wiring etc.), always DISCONNECT THE BATTERY.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected, the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will therefore remain released and free to turn.

## INTRODUCTION - INSTRUMENT/GAUGE ELECTRICAL CIRCUITS  [939010984]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5505 INSTRUMENT/GAUGE ELECTRICAL CIRCUITS > INTRODUCTION - INSTRUMENT/GAUGE ELECTRICAL CIRCUITS
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939010984

#### WARNINGS
> Note: This vehicle is equipped with a starting system known as Smart-Key, whereby the traditional ignition key has been replaced with an electronic key "T.E.G.", (Transmitter for Entry and Go), combined with an electronic key reader and TEG-Reader radio frequency receiver.
Access to the vehicle through radiofrequency recognition of the remote control incorporated into the TEG is entirely the same as what takes place with the traditional remote control incorporated into the key fob.
The vehicle is not switched on/started up by inserting the key in the ignition switch (which is no longer present) and turning it, but rather by inserting the TEG in the TEG Reader and activating the START/STOP button.
The distribution of the power supply to the vehicle (ignition-controlled INT, INT/A, AVV, etc.) is managed by the steering lock node and takes place in entirely the same way as for models equipped with a traditional key.
The following documentation often still uses the expressions Key ON, Key OFF, ignition-operated power supply (INT) or key to MAR, key to STOP, key to AVV. These must be interpreted in accordance with the following summary table:
| Key ON | TEG inserted in TEG Reader |
| --- | --- |
| Key OFF | TEG removed from TEG Reader |
| ignition controlled power supply (INT) | TEG inserted into TEG Reader |
| key on MAR | TEG inserted in TEG Reader |
| key on STOP | TEG removed from TEG Reader |
| key on AVV | TEG inserted and START/STOP button pressed |
To prevent serious damage to the electrical and electronic components, never try and start the engine with the battery earth lead slack.
Never pull the leads or wiring using force because this would result in the cables being disconnected from the connectors or terminals.
Disconnect all the control units and electronic devices when carrying out arc welding operations on the bodyshell.
Before replacing a fuse, check that the circuit protected is not faulty.
When replacing fuses, make sure that the new fuse is the correct amperage. Do not use fuses that have a higher or lower amperage than required.

#### PRECAUTIONS
When working on electrical components, always follow very carefully the precautions described below:
- Remove rings, wrist watches and other metal objects.
- Take care with electrical arcs: Even if the voltage is 12V, the battery can produce high currents if there is a short circuit, creating arcs or sparks that can be dangerous to the mechanic or create a fire hazard;
- Take care over high voltages. The electronic ignition system produces voltages of 20,000V and more, which could be very dangerous. Take great care when working on or near these components.
- Prevent fire risks. Do not smoke when working close to the battery or components which get hot during use (e.g. halogen bulbs), nor in the area of the engine compartment when the engine is still hot.
Before working on the electrical system (connectors, electrical components, wiring etc.), always DISCONNECT THE BATTERY.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected, the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will therefore remain released and free to turn.

## 5505A13 ADDITIONAL JUNCTION UNIT IN ENGINE COMPARTMENT - R.R.  [939010962]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5505 INSTRUMENT/GAUGE ELECTRICAL CIRCUITS > 5505A MULTI-FUNCTION COMPONENTS > 5505A13 ADDITIONAL JUNCTION UNIT IN ENGINE COMPARTMENT - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939010962

##### Removing
- Open the bonnet.
1. Undo the bolts (1a) and remove the cover (1b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001742.jpg]

1. Disconnect the upper electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| ENGINE COMPARTMENT JUNCTION UNIT | See B001 JUNCTION UNIT [→939003748] |
2. Undo the control unit side retainers.
3. Raise the control unit.
4. Release the control unit rotating pins from the retainers.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001743.jpg]

1. Disconnect the lower electrical connections (1a, 1b, 1c, 1d) for the control unit.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| ENGINE COMPARTMENT JUNCTION UNIT | See B001 JUNCTION UNIT [→939003748] |
2. Keep the control unit..
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001744.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the additional junction unit is not damaged.
- Connect the lower electrical connections and position the control unit in its seat.
- Do up the rotating pins and the control unit retainers.
- Connect the upper electrical connection.
- Close the cover and tighten the bolts.
- Close the bonnet.
- Test the operation of the electrical system.

## 5505A29 CONTAINER FOR ADDITIONAL JUNCTION UNIT IN ENGINE COMPARTMENT - R.R. WITH ADDITIONAL JUNCTION UNIT REMOVED  [939010961]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5505 INSTRUMENT/GAUGE ELECTRICAL CIRCUITS > 5505A MULTI-FUNCTION COMPONENTS > 5505A29 CONTAINER FOR ADDITIONAL JUNCTION UNIT IN ENGINE COMPARTMENT - R.R. WITH ADDITIONAL JUNCTION UNIT REMOVED
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939010961

##### Removing
1. Release the electrical connection for the retainer.
2. Release the duct (2a) and the electrical wiring (2b) from the container.
3. Undo the bolt (3a) and the nut (3b).
4. Remove the control unit container.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000765.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the additional junction unit container is not damaged.
- Place the container in position and secure it using the nut and the bolt.
- Fit the duct and the electrical wiring in place.
- Connect the electrical connection to the retainer.

## 5505A32 BODY COMPUTER - R.R  [939010966]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5505 INSTRUMENT/GAUGE ELECTRICAL CIRCUITS > 5505A MULTI-FUNCTION COMPONENTS > 5505A32 BODY COMPUTER - R.R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939010966

##### Removing
Op. 7040A37 FUSE BOX COVER IN INSTRUMENT PANEL - R.R [→939011243]
1. Release the retainers (1a) and separate the junction unit (1b) from the body computer (1c).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000767.jpg]

##### Refitting
Check that the body computer is not damaged.
At the bench, connect the junction unit to the body computer and attach the retainers.
If the body computer is being replaced, proceed as follows.
> Note: If the Body Computer is being replaced, an "identical copy of the Body Computer" must be obtained from the Parts Dept. as a V.O.R. order quoting the vehicle chassis number: this copy will contain all the default settings entered when the vehicle was new which are stored in the Parts Dept. database for the vehicle chassis number.
If the same body computer that was removed is being refitted on the vehicle, use the Examiner to check that the system has not picked up errors during the operation.
Op. 7040A37 FUSE BOX COVER IN INSTRUMENT PANEL - R.R [→939011243]
Test the operation of the electrical system.

- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939010969

##### Removing
1. Undo the bolts.
2. Disconnect the electrical connections (2a-b-c, 2d, 2e).
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| PASSENGER SIDE DOOR JUNCTION UNIT (NPP) | See M067 PASSENGER DOOR CONTROL UNIT (NPP) [→939004363] |
3. Keep the control unit..
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001746.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the control unit is not damaged.
- Connect the electrical connections for the control unit.
- Tighten the bolts.

## 5505B10 FRONT DOOR WIRING (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. WITH WATER GUARD OR FRAME DETACHED  [939010979]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5505 INSTRUMENT/GAUGE ELECTRICAL CIRCUITS > 5505B DOOR, BOOT AND BONNET WIRING > 5505B10 FRONT DOOR WIRING (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. WITH WATER GUARD OR FRAME DETACHED
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939010979

##### Removing
Op. 7005D24 FRONT DOOR WINDOW REAR GUIDE - R R [→939011130]
Op. 7005N20 FRONT SIDE DOOR LOCK MOTOR/CATCH ASSEMBLY, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R WITH DOOR PANEL AND SUPPORT FRAME REMOVED [→939011147]
1. Disconnect the door control unit electrical connections (1a-b-c, 1d).
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| DRIVER'S DOOR CONTROL UNIT (NPG) | See M066 DRIVER'S DOOR CONTROL UNIT (NPG) [→939004346] |
| PASSENGER SIDE DOOR JUNCTION UNIT (NPP) | See M067 PASSENGER DOOR CONTROL UNIT (NPP) [→939004363] |
2. Disconnect the electrical connection for the speaker.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| SPEAKER IN LEFT FRONT DOOR | See P035 SPEAKER ON FRONT DOOR LEFT [→939004577] |
| SPEAKER IN RIGHT FRONT DOOR | See P036 SPEAKER ON PASSENGER FRONT DOOR [→939004579] |
3. Disconnect the electrical connection for the window opening motor.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| LEFT FRONT ELECTRIC WINDOW MOTOR | See N060 DRIVER'S FRONT ELECTRIC WINDOW MOTOR [→939004434] |
| RIGHT FRONT ELECTRIC WINDOW MOTOR | See N061 RIGHT FRONT ELECTRIC WINDOW MOTOR [→939004437] |
4. Prise bulkhead coupling seals (4a and 3b) from the chassis.
5. Release the wiring retainers from the chassis.
6. Recover the wiring by withdrawing from the upper part.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001747.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the wiring is not damaged and that the individual conducts are correctly fastened to the connections.
- If necessary, carry out continuity tests.
- Position the wiring and attach the retainers.
- Fit the bulkhead coupling seals to the chassis.
- Connect the electrical connections.
Op. 7005N20 FRONT SIDE DOOR LOCK MOTOR/CATCH ASSEMBLY, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R WITH DOOR PANEL AND SUPPORT FRAME REMOVED [→939011147]
Op. 7005D24 FRONT DOOR WINDOW REAR GUIDE - R R [→939011130]

## 5505B20 REAR DOOR WIRING (ONE) LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R WITH WATER GUARD OR FRAME DETACHED  [939010983]

- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Low setup
##### Removing
1. Disconnect lock (1a) from retainers (1b).
2. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| LEFT REAR DOOR LOCK GEARED MOTOR | See N055 LEFT REAR DOOR LOCK GEARED MOTOR [→939004425] |
| RIGHT REAR DOOR LOCK GEARED MOTOR | See N056 RIGHT REAR DOOR LOCK GEARED MOTOR [→939004427] |
3. Prise bulkhead coupling seals (3a and 3b) from the chassis.
4. Tilt the chassis and disconnect the electrical connections.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| LEFT REAR SPEAKER | See P030 LEFT REAR SPEAKER [→939004569] |
| RIGHT REAR SPEAKER | See P031 RIGHT REAR SPEAKER [→939004572] |

6. Recover the wiring by withdrawing from the upper part.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001751.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Low setup
##### Refitting
- Check that the wiring is not damaged and that the individual conducts are correctly fastened to the connections.
- If necessary, carry out continuity tests.
- Position the wiring and attach the retainers.
- Fit the bulkhead coupling seals to the chassis.
- Connect the electrical connections.
- Connect the lock to the retainers.

## 5505B50 BOOT WIRING - R R  [939010982]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5505 INSTRUMENT/GAUGE ELECTRICAL CIRCUITS > 5505B DOOR, BOOT AND BONNET WIRING > 5505B50 BOOT WIRING - R R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939010982

##### Removing
Op. 7025B38 BOOT LID LOCK - R+R [→939011220]
1. Disconnect the electrical connections (1a, 1b).
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| LEFT REAR LIGHT CLUSTER (MOVING SECTION ON BOOT LID) | See F035 LEFT REAR LIGHT CLUSTER (MOVING SECTION ON BOOT LID) [→939004007] |
| RIGHT REAR LIGHT CLUSTER (MOVING SECTION ON BOOT LID) | See F036 RIGHT REAR LIGHT CLUSTER (MOVING SECTION ON BOOT LID) [→939004010] |
2. Release the wiring retainers.
3. Release the cable duct (3a) from the boot lid opening (3b).
4. Extract the wiring (4a) from the boot lid passing through the opening (3b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001748.jpg]

1. Release the cable duct (1a) from the body opening (1b).
2. Undo the retaining studs using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
3. Undo the nut.
4. Slightly lower the upper edge of the left boot trim.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001749.jpg]

1. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| REAR/BOOT COUPLING | See D040 REAR/BOOT COUPLING [→939003923] |
2. Withdraw the boot lid wiring from the body and recover.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001750.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the wiring is not damaged and that the individual conducts are correctly fastened to the connections.
If necessary, carry out continuity tests.
- Insert the wiring in the body.
- Connect the electrical connection.
- Position the upper edge of the left boot trim and secure with the retaining stud and nut.
- Fit the bulkhead coupling through the hole in the body.
- Reinsert the wiring into the boot lid.
- Fit the bulkhead coupling to hole in the boot lid.
- Connect the wiring retainers.
- Connect the electrical connections.
Op. 7025B38 BOOT LID LOCK - R+R [→939011220]
- Check that the lights in the dismantled parts are working.

## 5505B55 TAIL-GATE WIRING - R R  [939005344]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5505 INSTRUMENT/GAUGE ELECTRICAL CIRCUITS > 5505B DOOR, BOOT AND BONNET WIRING > 5505B55 TAIL-GATE WIRING - R R

- Applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Station Wagon
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939005344

##### Removing
Op. 7040G36 BULB HOLDER FOR LOAD CARRYING COMPARTMENT lighting on tailgate - R.R. [→939005460]
Op. 7015A05 TAILGATE LOWER INTERIOR PANEL-R R [→939005348]
Op. 7015A06 TAILGATE UPPER INTERIOR PANEL - R.R. [→939005349]
Op. 7210G76 REAR SPOILER - R.R. [→939005555]
- Work on the right side.
Op. 7040G40 LOAD COMPARTMENT LIGHT BULB HOLDER - R R [→939005461]
Op. 7040B22 LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT/LOAD PLATFORM MOULDING - R+R [→939011251]
Op. 7040B04 SIDE MOULDING (ONE) LH OR RH FOR LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT - R.R. [→939005451]
Op. 7040B10 LOAD PLATFORM (LUGGAGE PLATFORM) TRIM - R+R [→939011248]
Op. 7040B18 LOAD COMPARTMENT LOAD PLATFORM SIDE TRIM ONE LH OR RH - R.R [→939011249]
Op. 7040B30 L/H OR R/H SIDE SUPPORT (ONE) FOR LOAD PLATFORM COVER BLIND - ARRANGE [→939005454]
Op. 7040H42 REAR PILLAR TRIM (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT- R.R [→939011266]
Op. 7040H68 TAI GATE HOUSING PILLAR TRIM (ONE), LH OR RH - R.R. [→939005457]
- Remove the right rear grab handle.
Op. 7065C30 GRAB HANDLE (ONE) LEFT, RIGHT OR CENTRE PASSENGER COMPARTMENT INTERIOR - R.R [→939011375]
1. Release the roof lining rear retaining buttons using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005222.jpg]

1. Disconnect the electrical connections (1a, 1b, 1c, 1d).
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| REAR WIPER MOTOR | See N016 REAR WINDOW WIPER MOTOR [→939005539] |
| LEFT REAR LIGHT CLUSTER (MOVING SECTION ON BOOT LID) | See F035 LEFT REAR LIGHT CLUSTER (MOVING SECTION ON BOOT LID) [→939004007] |
| RIGHT REAR LIGHT CLUSTER (MOVING SECTION ON BOOT LID) | See F036 RIGHT REAR LIGHT CLUSTER (MOVING SECTION ON BOOT LID) [→939004010] |
| HEATED REAR WINDSCREEN | See P055 HEATED REARSCREEN [→939004602] |
| BOOT LOCK GEARED MOTOR | See N057 BOOT LOCK GEARED MOTOR [→939004429] |
| ADDITIONAL REAR LIGHT (THIRD BRAKE LIGHT) | See F040 ADDITIONAL TAIL-LIGHT ('THIRD BRAKE LIGHT') [→939004013] |
2. Disconnect the wiring retainers from the tailgate.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005223.jpg]

1. Disconnect the electrical connections (1a, 1b, 1c).
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| REAR / TAILGATE COUPLING | See D085 REAR/TAILGATE COUPLING [→939005515] |
2. Gently lower the roof lining (2a) and disconnect the retainers (2b) for the tailgate wiring from the bodyshell.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005224.jpg]

1. Undo nuts (1a) securing the duct seal (1b).
2. Release the duct seal (2a) and extract the wiring (2b) from the tailgate, passing it through the opening (2c).
3. Extract the rearscreen washer pipe (3a) from the connector (3b).
4. Extract the tailgate wiring (4a) from the bodyshell passing it through the opening (4b), pulling it upwards.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005225.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the wiring is not damaged and that the individual conducts are correctly fastened to the connections.
If necessary, carry out continuity tests.
- Fit the tailgate wiring in place, both in the bodyshell and the tailgate, then connect it to the retainers.
- Fit the rearscreen washer pipe in place in the connector.
- Attach the duct seals and tighten the nuts.
- Connect the electrical connections to the tailgate and to the bodyshell.
- Do up the rear ceiling retaining studs.

- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5505 INSTRUMENT/GAUGE ELECTRICAL CIRCUITS > 5505C VEHICLE COMPARTMENT WIRING > 5505C02 WIRING BUILT INTO FRONT BUMPER R R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)

- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939010973

##### Removing
Op. 7055B54 UNDER ENGINE PROTECTION/GUARD - R.R. [→939011369]
Op. 7210E10 R.R. FRONT BUMPER [→939011399]
1. Disconnect the electrical connections (1a) and (1b).
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| LEFT FOG LIGHT | See F015 LEFT FOG LAMP [→939003995] |
| RIGHT FOG LIGHT | See F016 RIGHT FOG LAMP [→939003997] |
| PARKING SENSOR UNIT | See K097 PARKING SENSORS [→939004246] |
2. Disconnect the wiring retainers.
3. Keep the bumper wiring.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004760.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the wiring is not damaged and that the individual conducts are correctly fastened to the connections.
If necessary, carry out continuity tests.
- Connect the wiring retainers.
- Connect the electrical connections.
Op. 7210E10 R.R. FRONT BUMPER [→939011399]
Op. 7055B54 UNDER ENGINE PROTECTION/GUARD - R.R. [→939011369]

## 5505C29 ENGINE COMPARTMENT AND A.B.S. BUILT-IN WIRING - R.R. WITH INTERFERING COMPONENTS REMOVED  [939010976]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5505 INSTRUMENT/GAUGE ELECTRICAL CIRCUITS > 5505C VEHICLE COMPARTMENT WIRING > 5505C29 ENGINE COMPARTMENT AND A.B.S. BUILT-IN WIRING - R.R. WITH INTERFERING COMPONENTS REMOVED
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939010976

##### Removing
> Note: See the Wiring Diagrams section for accurate identification of the electrical connections.
1. Working on both sides of the vehicle, undo the nuts (1a) securing bulkhead coupling node (1b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004106.jpg]

1. Disconnect all the electrical connections for the wiring.
2. Release the wiring from the retainers and remove it.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004107.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the wiring is not damaged and that the individual conducts are correctly fastened to the connections.
If necessary, carry out continuity tests.
- Position the wiring and secure it using the various retainers.
- Connect all the electrical connections for the wiring.
- Working on both sides of the windscreen base, position the bulkhead coupling node, then tighten the nuts.

## 5505C40 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT WIRING - R.R. WITH INTERFERING COMPONENTS DETACHED  [939010975]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5505 INSTRUMENT/GAUGE ELECTRICAL CIRCUITS > 5505C VEHICLE COMPARTMENT WIRING > 5505C40 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT WIRING - R.R. WITH INTERFERING COMPONENTS DETACHED
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939010975

##### Removing
> Note: See the Wiring Diagrams section for accurate identification of the electrical connections.
1. Working on the left side of the boot compartment, undo nuts (1a) and move the boot compartment junction unit (1b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004103.jpg]

1. Working the rear seat cushion area, unscrew bolts (1a) and move the access lid to fuel pump (1b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004104.jpg]

1. Disconnect all the electrical connections for the wiring.
2. Release the wiring from the retainers and remove it.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004105.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the wiring is not damaged and that the individual conducts are correctly fastened to the connections.
If necessary, carry out continuity tests.
- Position the wiring and secure it using the various retainers.
- Connect all the electrical connections for the wiring.
- Working in the rear seat cushion area, position the access lid to the fuel pump and tighten the bolts.
- Working on the left side of the boot compartment, position the boot junction unit and tighten the nuts.

## 5505C82 WIRING BUILT INTO REAR BUMPER R R  [939010974]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5505 INSTRUMENT/GAUGE ELECTRICAL CIRCUITS > 5505C VEHICLE COMPARTMENT WIRING > 5505C82 WIRING BUILT INTO REAR BUMPER R R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939010974

##### Removing
For the left and right sides.
Op. 5540A56 EXTERIOR REAR LIGHT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011023]
Op. 7210E66 R.R. REAR BUMPER [→939011398]
1. Disconnect the electrical connections (1a), (1b) and (1c).
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| LEFT NO. PLATE LIGHT | See F050 LEFT NUMBER PLATE LIGHT [→939004015] |
| RIGHT NO. PLATE LIGHT | See F051 RIGHT NUMBER PLATE LIGHT [→939004017] |
| PARKING SENSOR UNIT | See K097 PARKING SENSORS [→939004246] |
2. Disconnect the wiring retainers.
3. Keep the bumper wiring.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004761.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the wiring is not damaged and that the individual conducts are correctly fastened to the connections.
If necessary, carry out continuity tests.
- Connect the wiring retainers.
- Connect the electrical connections.
Op. 7210E66 R.R. REAR BUMPER [→939011398]
Op. 5540A56 EXTERIOR REAR LIGHT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011023]

## 5510C14 IGNITION COIL/S - R.R.  [939010988]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5510 ENGINE IGNITION > 5510C COIL, ECU, SENSORS > 5510C14 IGNITION COIL/S - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: engines: 1.9 JTS, 2.2 JTS, 3.2 JTS; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939010988

> Block applies to: engines: 1.9 JTS, 2.2 JTS; all production periods
##### Removing
- Make sure that the ignition key is in the OFF position, then disconnect the battery terminal (-).
|  | Description | Connector |
| --- | --- | --- |
| - | Battery | See A001 BATTERY [→939004879] |
- Undo the bolts and remove the coil cover.
1. Disconnect the electrical connection from the ignition coil module.
|  | Description | Connector |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Ignition coil | See A030 IGNITION COIL [→939003625] |
2. Undo the bolts (2a) and remove the ignition coil module (2b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005784.jpg]

> Block applies to: engines: 1.9 JTS, 2.2 JTS; all production periods
##### Refitting
- Fit the ignition coil module in place and secure it using the bolts.
- Connect the electrical connection of the ignition coil module.
- Place the coil cover back in its housing and secure it using the bolts.
- Connect the negative battery terminal.

> Block applies to: engines: 3.2 JTS; all production periods
- To remove/refit the left cylinder head ignition coils,
Op. 5510C16 LEFT HAND CYLINDER HEAD IGNITION COIL - R+R [→939005331]
- To remove/refit the right cylinder head ignition coils,
Op. 5510C18 RIGHT HAND CYLINDER HEAD IGNITION COIL - R+R [→939005332]

## 5510C16 LEFT HAND CYLINDER HEAD IGNITION COIL - R+R  [939005331]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5510 ENGINE IGNITION > 5510C COIL, ECU, SENSORS > 5510C16 LEFT HAND CYLINDER HEAD IGNITION COIL - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: engines: 3.2 JTS; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939005331

##### Removing
Op. 1016A10 SOUND-PROOFING COVER - R.R [→939010416]
1. Disconnect the electrical connections for the left cylinder head ignition coils.
|  | Description | Connector |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Ignition coil | See A030 IGNITION COIL [→939003625] |
2. Undo the bolts (2a) and remove the ignition coils (2b) from the left cylinder head.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/946001274.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Place the ignition coils in their housings on the left cylinder head and tighten the bolts to the recommended torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Ignition coils | Bolt |  | 1.0 | 3.2 JTS |
- Connect the electrical connections for the left cylinder head ignition coils.
Op. 1016A10 SOUND-PROOFING COVER - R.R [→939010416]

## 5510C18 RIGHT HAND CYLINDER HEAD IGNITION COIL - R+R  [939005332]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5510 ENGINE IGNITION > 5510C COIL, ECU, SENSORS > 5510C18 RIGHT HAND CYLINDER HEAD IGNITION COIL - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: engines: 3.2 JTS; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939005332

##### Removing
Op. 1016A10 SOUND-PROOFING COVER - R.R [→939010416]
Op. 1072B50 AIR CHAMBER - R.R. [→939010591]
1. Disconnect the electrical connections for the right cylinder head ignition coils.
|  | Description | Connector |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Ignition coil | See A030 IGNITION COIL [→939003625] |
2. Undo the fixing bolts and remove the ignition coils for the right cylinder head.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/946001275.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Replace the right cylinder head ignition coils in their housings and secure them tightening the bolts to the recommended torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Ignition coils | Bolt |  | 1.0 | 3.2 JTS |
- Connect the electrical connections for the right cylinder head ignition coils.
Op. 1072B50 AIR CHAMBER - R.R. [→939010591]
Op. 1016A10 SOUND-PROOFING COVER - R.R [→939010416]

## 5510C26 ENGINE RPM SENSOR - R+R  [939010989]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5510 ENGINE IGNITION > 5510C COIL, ECU, SENSORS > 5510C26 ENGINE RPM SENSOR - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: engines: 1.9 JTS, 2.2 JTS, 3.2 JTS; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939010989

> Block applies to: engines: 1.9 JTS, 2.2 JTS; all production periods
##### Removing
1. Disconnect the electrical connection for the rpm sensor.
|  | Description | Connector |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Rpm sensor | See K046 RPM SENSOR [→939004195] |
2. Undo the bolt (2a) and remove the rpm sensor (2b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005785.jpg]

> Block applies to: engines: 1.9 JTS, 2.2 JTS; all production periods
##### Refitting
- Position the rpm sensor in its housing and secure it using the bolt.
- Connect the electrical connection for the rpm sensor.

> Block applies to: engines: 3.2 JTS; all production periods
> Note: This operation can only be carried out with the power unit removed, therefore refer to:Op. 1004B10 POWER UNIT WITH MANUAL GEARBOX - R.R [→939010395]Op. 1004D10 POWER UNIT (MANUAL GEARBOX), REMOVED, DISMANTLE AND REASSEMBLE [→939010397]Op. 1004D40 ENGINE - POSITION ON STAND AND REMOVE [→939010398]Op. 1004E10 ENGINE, REMOVED - REMOVE CYLINDER HEAD AND OIL SUMP FOR INSPECTION - INCLUDES POSITIONING ON STAND AND REMOVAL [→939010400]Op. 1004E20 ENGINE - DISMANTLE AND REASSEMBLE FOLLOWING OPERATION 1004E10 - WASH AND CHECK DISMANTLED PARTS - REFIT CYLINDER HEAD AND OIL SUMP - DOES NOT INCLUDE REPAIRS TO CYLINDER HEAD AND AUXILIARY UNIT [→939010401]

Op. 1080B92 LEFT UPPER LAMBDA SENSOR - R.R. [→939010620]

##### Removing
- Make sure that the ignition key is in the OFF position, then disconnect the battery terminal (-).
|  | Description | Connector |
| --- | --- | --- |
| - | Battery | See A001 BATTERY [→939004879] |
1. Disconnect the electrical connection (1a) and undo and remove the detonation sensor (1b).
|  | Description | Connector |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1a | Detonation sensor - 2 | See K050 DETONATION SENSOR [→939004203] |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004111.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Place the detonation sensor back in its housing, connect the connection and tighten the bolt to the recommended torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Detonation sensor | Bolt | M10 | 1.6 ÷ 2.0 | 1.9 JTS 2.2 JTS |
- Connect the negative battery terminal.

| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Ignition coils | Bolt |  | 1.0 | 3.2 JTS |
- Connect the electrical connections for the ignition coils.
- Place the ignition coil cover on the left cylinder head back in its housing and secure it using the bolts.

## ENGINE IGNITION - GENERAL REMARKS  [939010994]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5510 ENGINE IGNITION > ENGINE IGNITION - GENERAL REMARKS
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: engines: 1.9 JTS, 2.2 JTS; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939010994

#### WARNINGS
> Note: This vehicle is equipped with a starting system known as Smart-Key, whereby the traditional ignition key has been replaced with an electronic key, "T.E.G." (Transmitter for Entry and Go), combined with an electronic key reader and TEG-Reader radio frequency receiver.
The vehicle is not switched on/started up by inserting the key in the ignition switch (which is no longer present) and turning it, but rather by inserting the TEG into the TEG Reader and activating the START/STOP button.
Access to the vehicle through radiofrequency recognition of the remote control incorporated into the TEG is entirely the same as what takes place with the traditional remote control built into the key fob.
The distribution of the power supply to the vehicle (ignition-controlled INT, INT/A, AVV, etc.) is managed by the steering lock node and takes place in entirely the same way as for models equipped with a traditional key.
The following documentation often still uses the expressions Key ON, Key OFF, ignition-operated power supply (INT) or key to MAR, key to STOP, key to AVV. These must be interpreted according to the following summary table:
| Key ON | TEG inserted in TEG Reader |
| --- | --- |
| Key OFF | TEG removed from TEG Reader |
| ignition controlled power supply (INT) | TEG inserted into TEG Reader |
| key on MAR | TEG inserted in TEG Reader |
| key on STOP | TEG removed from TEG Reader |
| key on AVV | TEG inserted and START/STOP button pressed |
To prevent serious damage to the electrical and electronic components, never try and start the engine with the battery earth lead slack.
Never pull the leads or wiring using force because this would result in the cables being disconnected from the connectors or terminals.
Disconnect all the control units and electronic devices when carrying out arc welding operations on the bodyshell.
Before replacing a fuse, check that the circuit protected is not faulty.
When replacing fuses, make sure that the new fuse is the correct amperage. Do not use fuses that have a higher or lower amperage than required.

#### PRECAUTIONS
Take care with high voltages. The electronic ignition system produces voltages of 20,000V and more, which could be very dangerous. Take great care when working on or near these components.
When working on electrical components, always follow very carefully the precautions described below:
- Remove rings, wrist watches and other metal objects.
- Prevent fire risks. Do not smoke when working close to the battery or components which get hot during use (e.g. halogen bulbs), nor in the area of the engine compartment when the engine is still hot.
Before working on the electrical system (connectors, electrical components, wiring etc.), always DISCONNECT THE BATTERY.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected, the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will therefore remain released and free to turn.

## INTRODUCTION - ENGINE STARTING  [939011005]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5520 ENGINE STARTING > INTRODUCTION - ENGINE STARTING
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011005

#### WARNINGS

##### Steering lock
Since no mechanical key is present, the steering lock function is performed electrically via the steering lock node.
The steering lock node releases the steering when the ignition-operated services are activated. INT, i.e. when a known TEG is identified in the TEG Reader
The locking of the steering by the steering lock node takes place when the TEG is extracted from the TEG Reader
> Note: In the event of emergency TEG removal, the steering is NOT locked. Similarly, if the TEG is removed when the vehicle speed is greater than 4 km, the steering is NOT locked.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected, the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will therefore remain released and free to turn.
> Note: This vehicle is equipped with a starting system known as Smart-Key, whereby the traditional ignition key has been replaced with an electronic key, "T.E.G." (Transmitter for Entry and Go), combined with an electronic key reader and TEG-Reader radio frequency receiver.
Access to the vehicle through radiofrequency recognition of the remote control incorporated into the TEG is entirely the same as what takes place with the traditional remote control incorporated into the key fob.
The vehicle is not switched on/started up by inserting the key in the ignition switch (which is no longer present) and turning it, but rather by inserting the TEG into the TEG Reader and activating the START/STOP button.
The distribution of the power supply to the vehicle (ignition-controlled INT, INT/A, AVV, etc.) is managed by the steering lock node and takes place in entirely the same way as for models equipped with a traditional key.
The following documentation often still uses the expressions Key ON, Key OFF, ignition-operated power supply (INT) or key to MAR, key to STOP, key to AVV. These must be interpreted in accordance with the following summary table:
| Key ON | TEG inserted in TEG Reader |
| --- | --- |
| Key OFF | TEG removed from TEG Reader |
| ignition controlled power supply (INT) | TEG inserted into TEG Reader |
| key on MAR | TEG inserted in TEG Reader |
| key on STOP | TEG removed from TEG Reader |
| key on AVV | TEG inserted and START/STOP button pressed |
To prevent serious damage to the electrical and electronic components, never try and start the engine with the battery earth lead slack.
Never pull the leads or wiring using force because this would result in the cables being disconnected from the connectors or terminals.
Disconnect all the control units and electronic devices when carrying out arc welding operations on the bodyshell.
Before replacing a fuse, check that the circuit protected is not faulty.
When replacing fuses, make sure that the new fuse is the correct amperage. Do not use fuses that have a higher or lower amperage than required.

#### PRECAUTIONS
When working on electrical components, always follow very carefully the precautions described below:
- Remove rings, wrist watches and other metal objects.
- Take care with electrical arcs: Even if the voltage is 12V, the battery can produce high currents if there is a short circuit, creating arcs or sparks that can be dangerous to the mechanic or create a fire hazard;
- Take care over high voltages. The electronic ignition system produces voltages of 20,000V and more, which could be very dangerous. Take great care when working on or near these components.
- Prevent fire risks. Do not smoke when working close to the battery or components which get hot during use (e.g. halogen bulbs), nor in the area of the engine compartment when the engine is still hot.
Before working on the electrical system (connectors, electrical components, wiring etc.), always DISCONNECT THE BATTERY.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected, the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will therefore remain released and free to turn.

## 5520B10 STARTER MOTOR - R+R  [939010997]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5520 ENGINE STARTING > 5520B STARTER MOTOR AND COMPONENTS > 5520B10 STARTER MOTOR - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: engines: 1.9 JTS, 2.2 JTS, 3.2 JTS, 1.9 JTD 8V, 1.9 JTD 16V, 2.4 JTD 20V; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939010997

> Block applies to: engines: 1.9 JTD 8V, 1.9 JTD 16V; all production periods
##### Removing
- Position the vehicle on a lift.
- Raise the vehicle.
Op. 7055B54 UNDER ENGINE PROTECTION/GUARD - R.R. [→939011369]
1. Undo the nuts (1a) and release the bracket (1b) for the starter motor (1c).
2. Open the band (2a) and move the pipe (2b) from the oil vapour separator to the oil sump.
3. Open the protective cover and disconnect the electrical connections (3a and 3b) for the starter motor.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| STARTER MOTOR | See A020 STARTER MOTOR [→939003234] |
4. Undo the bolts securing the starter motor.
5. Remove the starter motor.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000804.jpg]

> Block applies to: engines: 1.9 JTD 8V, 1.9 JTD 16V; all production periods
##### Refitting
- Check that the starter motor is not damaged.
- Place the starter motor in position.
- Tighten the starter motor fixing bolts to the specified torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| STARTER MOTOR | Bolt | M8x45 | 2.2 ÷ 2.6 |  |
- Connect the electrical connections for the starter motor and close the protective cover.
- Place the pipe from the oil vapour separator to the oil sump in position and secure it using the band.
- Place the starter motor bracket in position and secure it using the nuts.
- Lower the vehicle.
- Remove the vehicle from the lift.

> Block applies to: engines: 1.9 JTS, 2.2 JTS; all production periods
##### Removing
- Position the vehicle on a lift.
Op. 7055B54 UNDER ENGINE PROTECTION/GUARD - R.R. [→939011369]
1. Open the protective cover and detach electrical connections (1a, 1b) for the starter motor.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| STARTER MOTOR | See A020 STARTER MOTOR [→939003234] |
2. Undo the bolts securing the starter motor.
3. Remove the starter motor.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001800.jpg]

> Block applies to: engines: 1.9 JTS, 2.2 JTS; all production periods
##### Refitting
- Check that the starter motor is not damaged.
1. Place the starter motor in position.
2. Tighten the starter motor fixing bolts to the specified torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| STARTER MOTOR | Bolt | M8x45 | 2.2 ÷ 2.6 |  |
3. Secure the electrical connections for the starter motor and close the protective cover.
Op. 7055B54 UNDER ENGINE PROTECTION/GUARD - R.R. [→939011369]
- Lower the vehicle.
- Remove the vehicle from the lift.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001801.jpg]

> Block applies to: engines: 2.4 JTD 20V; all production periods
##### Removing
- Position the vehicle on a lift.
Op. 7210E20 R.R. BONNET [→939011400]
1. Undo nuts (1a) and move vacuum pump (1b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004112.jpg]

> Block applies to: engines: 2.4 JTD 20V; all production periods
Op. 7055B54 UNDER ENGINE PROTECTION/GUARD - R.R. [→939011369]
1. Detach the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| INSUFFICIENT ENGINE OIL PRESSURE SENSOR | See K030 LOW ENGINE OIL PRESSURE SENSOR (SWITCH) [→939004179] |
2. Disconnect pipe (2a) from oil vapour separator (2b).
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| STARTER MOTOR | See A020 STARTER MOTOR [→939003234] |
4. Undo the bolts securing the starter motor.
5. Remove the starter motor.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004113.jpg]

> Block applies to: engines: 2.4 JTD 20V; all production periods

- Check that the starter motor is not damaged.
1. Place the starter motor in position.
2. Tighten the starter motor fixing bolts to the specified torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| STARTER MOTOR | Bolt | M8x45 | 2.2 ÷ 2.6 |  |
3. Secure electrical connections (3a and 3b) for the starter motor and reclose the protective cover.
4. Connect pipe (4a) to oil vapour separator (4b).
5. Secure the electrical connection for the engine oil pressure sensor.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004114.jpg]

> Block applies to: engines: 2.4 JTD 20V; all production periods
Op. 7055B54 UNDER ENGINE PROTECTION/GUARD - R.R. [→939011369]

Op. 7210E20 R.R. BONNET [→939011400]

1. Undo the bolts.

- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011016

#### WARNINGS
> Note: This vehicle is equipped with a starting system known as Smart-Key, where the traditional ignition key has been replaced with an electronic key "T.E.G." (Transmitter for Entry and Go), combined with an electronic key reader and TEG-Reader radio frequency receiver.
Access to the vehicle through radiofrequency recognition of the remote control built into the TEG is entirely the same as what takes place with the traditional remote control incorporated into the key fob.
The vehicle is not switched on/started up by inserting the key in the ignition switch (which is no longer present) and turning it but by inserting the TEG in the TEG Reader and activating the START/STOP button.
The distribution of the power supply to the vehicle (ignition-controlled INT, INT/A, AVV, etc.) is managed by the steering lock node and takes place in entirely the same way as for models equipped with a traditional key.
The following documentation often still uses the expressions Key ON, Key OFF, ignition-operated power supply (INT) or key to MAR, key to STOP, key to AVV. These must be interpreted in accordance with the following summary table:
| Key ON | TEG inserted in TEG Reader |
| --- | --- |
| Key OFF | TEG removed from TEG Reader |
| ignition controlled power supply (INT) | TEG inserted into TEG Reader |
| key on MAR | TEG inserted in TEG Reader |
| key on STOP | TEG removed from TEG Reader |
| key on AVV | TEG inserted and START/STOP button pressed |
To prevent serious damage to the electrical and electronic components, never try and start the engine with the battery earth lead slack.
Never pull the leads or wiring using force because this would result in the cables being disconnected from the connectors or terminals.
Disconnect all the control units and electronic devices when carrying out arc welding operations on the bodyshell.
Before replacing a fuse, check that the circuit protected is not faulty.
When replacing fuses, make sure that the new fuse is the correct amperage. Do not use fuses that have a higher or lower amperage than required.

#### PRECAUTIONS
When working on electrical components, always follow very carefully the precautions described below:
- Remove rings, wrist watches and other metal objects.
- Take care with electrical arcs: Even if the voltage is 12V, the battery can produce high currents if there is a short circuit, creating arcs or sparks that can be dangerous to the mechanic or create a fire hazard;
- Take care over high voltages. The electronic ignition system produces voltages of 20,000V and more, which could be very dangerous. Take great care when working on or near these components.
- Prevent fire risks. Do not smoke when working close to the battery or components which get hot during usage (e.g. halogen bulbs) or in the area of the engine compartment when the engine is still hot.
Before working on the electrical system (connectors, electrical components, wiring etc.), always DISCONNECT THE BATTERY.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected, the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will therefore remain released and free to turn.

## 5530A24 ALTERNATOR VOLTAGE REGULATOR - R.R. WITH ALTERNATOR REMOVED  [939011015]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5530 CURRENT GENERATION > 5530A ALTERNATOR AND COMPONENTS > 5530A24 ALTERNATOR VOLTAGE REGULATOR - R.R. WITH ALTERNATOR REMOVED
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: engines: 1.9 JTS, 2.2 JTS, 3.2 JTS, 1.9 JTD 8V, 1.9 JTD 16V, 2.4 JTD 20V; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011015

##### Removing
1. Undo both nuts.
2. Undo the bolt.
3. Recover the rear alternator cover.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001821.jpg]

1. Unscrew and remove both bolts.
2. Recover the alternator voltage governor.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001822.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the voltage governor is not damaged.
- Thoroughly clean the removed parts.
- Place the voltage governor in position and tighten the two fixing bolts.
- Position the cover and secure with the bolt and both nuts.

## 5530B10 BATTERY - R+R  [939011011]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5530 CURRENT GENERATION > 5530B BATTERY AND LEADS > 5530B10 BATTERY - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011011

##### Removing
- Open the bonnet.
1. Disconnect the negative terminal (1a) from the battery after having loosened it by rotating the cam lever (1b).
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| BATTERY | See A001 BATTERY [→939004879] |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001806.jpg]

1. Release the retainers (1a) and remove the cover (1b) for the supply box.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001807.jpg]

1. Undo the bolts.
2. Remove the retaining bracket.
3. Loosen the nut for the positive battery terminal.
4. Move the supply box to the side.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001808.jpg]

1. Undo the nut (1a) and move the battery retaining belt (1b).
2. Remove the battery.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001809.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the battery is not damaged.
- Place the battery in its housing.
- Position the retaining belt on the battery and tighten the nut.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| BATTERY RETAINING BELT | Nut | M8 | 1.2 ÷ 1.8 |  |
1. Position the supply box.
2. Fasten the positive battery terminal using the nut.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| BATTERY POSITIVE TERMINAL | Nut | M6 | 0.35 ÷ 0.55 |  |
3. Place the retaining bracket (3a) in position and tighten the bolts (3b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001810.jpg]

- Place the supply box cover in position and attach the retainers.
- Place the negative battery terminal in position, then secure it using the cam lever.
- Close the bonnet.
- Test the operation of the electrical system.

| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |

- Place the negative battery terminal in position, then secure it using the cam lever.
- Close the bonnet.
- Test the operation of the electrical system.

## 5530B40 SUPPLY BOX ON BATTERY (LINK BATTERY AND FUSE BOX) - R R  [939011008]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5530 CURRENT GENERATION > 5530B BATTERY AND LEADS > 5530B40 SUPPLY BOX ON BATTERY (LINK BATTERY AND FUSE BOX) - R R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011008

##### Removing
- Open the bonnet.
1. Release the retainers (1a) and remove the cover (1b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001802.jpg]

1. Disconnect the electrical connections (1a, 1b, 1c, 1e, 1f).
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| MAXI FUSE BOX ON BATTERY | See B099 MAXIFUSE BOX ON BATTERY [→939003783] |
2. Undo the bolts.
3. Remove the retaining bracket.
4. Loosen the nut for the positive battery terminal.
5. Remove the supply box.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001803.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the supply box is not damaged.
1. Place the supply box in position and attach the retainers.
2. Fasten the positive battery terminal using the nut.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| BATTERY POSITIVE TERMINAL | Nut | M6 | 0.35 ÷ 0.55 |  |
3. Place the retaining bracket (3a) in position and tighten the bolts (3b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001804.jpg]

- Connect the electrical connections.
- Close the cover and attach the retainers.
- Close the bonnet.
- Test the operation of the electrical system

## INTRODUCTION - EXTERIOR LIGHTING  [939011044]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5540 EXTERIOR LIGHTING > INTRODUCTION - EXTERIOR LIGHTING
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011044

#### WARNINGS
> Note: This vehicle is equipped with a starting system known as Smart-Key, where the traditional ignition key has been replaced with an electronic key "T.E.G." (Transmitter for Entry and Go), combined with an electronic key reader and TEG-Reader radio frequency receiver.
Access to the vehicle through radiofrequency recognition of the remote control built into the TEG is entirely the same as what takes place with the traditional remote control incorporated in the key fob.
The vehicle is not switched on/started up by inserting the key in the ignition switch (which is no longer present) and turning it, but rather by inserting the TEG into the TEG Reader and activating the START/STOP button.
The distribution of the power supply to the vehicle (ignition-controlled INT, INT/A, AVV, etc.) is managed by the steering lock node and takes place in exactly the same way as for models equipped with a traditional key.
The following documentation often still uses the expressions Key ON, Key OFF, ignition-operated power supply (INT) or key to MAR, key to STOP, key to AVV. These must be interpreted in accordance with the following summary table:
| Key ON | TEG inserted in TEG Reader |
| --- | --- |
| Key OFF | TEG removed from TEG Reader |
| ignition controlled power supply (INT) | TEG inserted into TEG Reader |
| key on MAR | TEG inserted in TEG Reader |
| key on STOP | TEG removed from TEG Reader |
| key on AVV | TEG inserted and START/STOP button pressed |
To prevent serious damage to the electrical and electronic components, never try and start the engine with the battery earth lead slack.
Never pull the leads or wiring using force because this would result in the cables being disconnected from the connectors or terminals.
Disconnect all the control units and electronic devices when carrying out arc welding operations on the bodyshell.
Before replacing a fuse, check that the circuit protected is not faulty.
When replacing fuses, make sure that the new fuse is the correct amperage. Do not use fuses that have a higher or lower amperage than required.

#### PRECAUTIONS
When working on electrical components, always follow very carefully the precautions described below:
- Remove rings, wrist watches and other metal objects.
- Take care with electrical arcs: even if the voltage is 12V, the battery can produce high currents if there is a short circuit, creating arcs or sparks that can be dangerous to the mechanic or create a fire hazard;
- Take care over high voltages. the electronic ignition system produces voltages of 20,000V and more which could be very dangerous. Take great care when working on or near these components.
- Prevent fire risks. Do not smoke when working close to the battery or components which get hot during use (e.g. halogen bulbs), nor in the area of the engine compartment when the engine is still hot.
Before working on the electrical system (connectors, electrical components, wiring etc.), always DISCONNECT THE BATTERY.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected, the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will therefore remain released and free to turn.

## 5540A56 EXTERIOR REAR LIGHT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.  [939011023]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5540 EXTERIOR LIGHTING > 5540A LIGHTING > 5540A56 EXTERIOR REAR LIGHT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011023

##### Removing
- Open the boot lid.
1. Undo the nut fixing the inner light cover.
2. Remove the inner light cover (2a) releasing the retainer (2b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000798.jpg]

1. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| LEFT REAR LIGHT CLUSTER | See F030 LEFT REAR LIGHT CLUSTER [→939004003] |
| RIGHT REAR LIGHT CLUSTER | See F031 RIGHT REAR LIGHT CLUSTER [→939004005] |
2. Undo the light retaining nuts.
3. Remove the light from the outside.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000799.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the light is not damaged.
- Position the light in the housing.
- Tighten the nuts.
- Connect the electrical connection.
- Position the inner light cover and attach the retainer.
- Tighten the nut.
- Close the boot lid.
- Check that the outer rear light cluster lights are working.

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Station Wagon
##### Removing
- Open the tailgate.
1. Remove the luggage compartment side door (1a) using the grip (1b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005226.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Station Wagon
1. Remove the protective inner light cover (1a) releasing the retainers (1b).
2. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| LEFT REAR LIGHT CLUSTER | See F030 LEFT REAR LIGHT CLUSTER [→939004003] |
| RIGHT REAR LIGHT CLUSTER | See F031 RIGHT REAR LIGHT CLUSTER [→939004005] |
3. Undo the light retaining nuts.
4. Remove the light from the outside.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005227.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Station Wagon
##### Refitting
- Check that the light is not damaged.
- Position the light in the housing.
- Tighten the nuts.
- Connect the electrical connection.
- Place the protective inner light cover in position and attach the retainers.
- Fit the luggage compartment side cover in place.
- Close the tailgate.
- Check that the outer rear light cluster lights are working.

## 5540B39 DIPPED HEADLAMP LIGHT BEAM - ALIGN  [939011037]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5540 EXTERIOR LIGHTING > 5540B HEADLAMPS > 5540B39 DIPPED HEADLAMP LIGHT BEAM - ALIGN
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011037

- Position the vehicle complete with spare wheel, tools, fluids, fuel reserve and with the driver on board.
- Check that the tyre inflation pressure is correct.
- Place the headlamp alignment control switch in position 0.
- Place the vehicle on a flat surface, perpendicular to a screen with an opaque surface 10 metres away on which the following lines are traced.
1. V-V: vertical, corresponding to the trace of vehicle plane of symmetry.
2. C-C: traces of vertical planes passing through the reference centres of the light clusters.
3. HC-HC: horizontal corresponding to the height from the ground of the reference centres for the light clusters.
4. AC-AC: horizontal 10 cm below the line HC - HC.
5. Lines inclined by 15° as illustrated in the diagram.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001791.jpg]

- Switch on the dipped headlamps.
1. Vertical alignment. Adjust the vertical adjustment screw for the light cluster so that the horizontal section of the demarcation line between the dark zone and the area lit up by the light band is aligned with the line AC - AC traced on the screen.
2. Horizontal alignment. Adjust the horizontal adjustment screw for the light cluster so that the point where the two horizontal and inclined demarcation lines cross with the point where lines C - C and AC - AC cross on the screen are aligned.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004122.jpg]

## 5540B40 CONTROL UNIT IN LEFT GAS DISCHARGE LIGHT CLUSTER - R.R.  [939004917]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5540 EXTERIOR LIGHTING > 5540B HEADLAMPS > 5540B40 CONTROL UNIT IN LEFT GAS DISCHARGE LIGHT CLUSTER - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939004917

##### Removing
Op. 5540B81 LEFT GAS DISCHARGE LIGHT CLUSTER - R.R. [→939004921]
1. Loosen the control unit retaining bolts.
2. Remove control unit (2a), disconnecting electrical connections (2b) and (2c).
3. If necessary, undo the bolts (3a) and remove the control unit mount (3b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/946000954.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the control unit is not damaged.
1. If removed from mount (1a), check that seal (1b) and the support surface on mount (1a) are clean and efficient before refitting.
2. Fit mount (1a) in place and secure it using the bolts.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/946000955.jpg]

- Position the control unit in its seat and do up the electrical connections.
- Tighten the control unit bolts.
Op. 5540B81 LEFT GAS DISCHARGE LIGHT CLUSTER - R.R. [→939004921]

##### Removing
- Open the bonnet.
- Proceed as follows to replace the high beam bulb.
1. Remove the cover (bayonet mount).
2. Disconnect the electrical connection for the bulb.
3. Release the bulb retaining clips by pushing in the arrowed direction.
4. Extract the bulb from the bulb holder.
> Note: If the bulb is working properly, avoid touching it with your hands. If this does happen, clean the bulb with alcohol.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001782.jpg]

- Proceed as follows to replace the turn signal bulb.
1. Remove the cover (bayonet mount).
2. Extract the bulb holder and bulb (bayonet fitting).
3. Extract the bulb from the bulb holder (bayonet fitting).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001783.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check the condition of the bulb and make sure that it is clean.
- Proceed as follows to replace the high beam bulb.
> Note: Avoid touching the bulb with your hands. If this does happen, clean the bulb with alcohol.
1. Fit the bulb in the bulb holder. The correct position for the bulb is illustrated.
- Do up the bulb retaining clips.
- Connect the electrical connection for the bulb.
- Refit the cover (bayonet mount).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001784.jpg]

- Proceed as follows to replace the turn signal bulb.
- Fit the bulb in the bulb holder (bayonet mount).
- If necessary, fit the bulb holder and bulb in the housing (bayonet mount).
- Refit the cover (bayonet mount).
- Close the bonnet lid when the operation is complete.
- Check that the bulb is working.

- Position the vehicle complete with spare wheel, tools, fluids, fuel reserve.
- Check that the tyre inflation pressure is correct.
- Position the vehicle on a flat surface, perpendicular to a screen or opaque surface 10 metres away on which a line corresponding to the height from the ground (A) of the centre of the fog lights has been traced.

## INTRODUCTION - WARNING AND MANOUEVRING SIGNALS  [939011060]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5550 WARNING AND MANOEUVRING INDICATORS > INTRODUCTION - WARNING AND MANOUEVRING SIGNALS
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011060

#### WARNINGS
> Note: This vehicle is equipped with a starting system known as Smart-Key, whereby the traditional ignition key has been replaced with an electronic key "T.E.G.", (Transmitter for Entry and Go), combined with an electronic key reader and TEG-Reader radio frequency receiver.
The vehicle is not switched on/started up by inserting the key in the ignition switch (which is no longer present) and turning it, but rather by inserting the TEG in the TEG Reader and activating the START/STOP button.
Access to the vehicle through radiofrequency recognition of the remote control incorporated into the TEG is entirely the same as what takes place with the traditional remote control incorporated into the key fob.
The distribution of the power supply to the vehicle (ignition-controlled INT, INT/A, AVV, etc.) is managed by the steering lock node and takes place in entirely the same way as for models equipped with a traditional key.
The following documentation often still uses the expressions Key ON, Key OFF, ignition-operated power supply (INT) or key to MAR, key to STOP, key to AVV. These must be interpreted in accordance with the following summary table:
| Key ON | TEG inserted in TEG Reader |
| --- | --- |
| Key OFF | TEG removed from TEG Reader |
| ignition controlled power supply (INT) | TEG inserted into TEG Reader |
| key on MAR | TEG inserted in TEG Reader |
| key on STOP | TEG removed from TEG Reader |
| key on AVV | TEG inserted and START/STOP button pressed |
To prevent serious damage to the electrical and electronic components, never try and start the engine with the battery earth lead slack.
Never pull the leads or wiring using force because this would result in the cables being disconnected from the connectors or terminals.
Disconnect all the control units and electronic devices when carrying out arc welding operations on the bodyshell.
Before replacing a fuse, check that the circuit protected is not faulty.
When replacing fuses, make sure that the new fuse is the correct amperage. - Do not use fuses that have a higher or lower amperage than required.

#### PRECAUTIONS
When working on electrical components, always follow very carefully the precautions described below:
- Remove rings, wrist watches and other metal objects.
- Take care with electrical arcs: Even if the voltage is 12V, the battery can produce high currents if there is a short circuit, creating arcs or sparks that can be dangerous to the mechanic or create a fire hazard;
- Take care over high voltages. The electronic ignition system produces voltages of 20,000V and more, which could be very dangerous. Take great care when working on or near these components.
- Prevent fire risks. Do not smoke when working close to the battery or components which get hot during use (e.g. halogen bulbs), nor in the area of the engine compartment when the engine is still hot.
Before working on the electrical system (connectors, electrical components, wiring etc.), always DISCONNECT THE BATTERY.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected, the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will therefore remain released and free to turn.

## 5550A10 STALK UNIT ASSEMBLY - R+R  [939011059]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5550 WARNING AND MANOEUVRING INDICATORS > 5550A STALK UNIT > 5550A10 STALK UNIT ASSEMBLY - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011059

##### Removing
Op. 7040A26 STEERING COLUMN TRIM - R+R [→939011231]
> Note: Follow the safety instructions for this operation meticulously
Op. 5580C18 AIR BAG MODULE - R+R [→939011097]
Op. 4110A10 STEERING WHEEL - R+R FOR REPLACEMENT OR CHECKS [→939010817]
1. Disconnect the electrical connection for the clock spring (1a) and the steering sensor (1b).
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| CLOCK SPRING COUPLING | See D047 CLOCK SPRING COUPLING [→939003930] |
| STEERING SENSOR | See K058 STEERING SENSOR [→939004215] |
2. Disconnect the electrical connections (2a, 2b, 2c).
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| STEERING COLUMN SWITCH UNIT | See H005 STEERING COLUMN SWITCH UNIT [→939004063] |
3. Loosen the steering column switch unit retaining band.
4. Remove the steering column switch unit assembly (4a) releasing the tab (4b) from the steering column retainer.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001772.jpg]

> Note: Neither the clock spring, nor the steering angle sensor, nor the Cruise Control system control can be separated from the steering column switch unit. Avoid carrying out operations on the individual components.

##### Refitting
- Check that the steering column switch unit is not damaged.
- Check that the wheels are straight.
- Fit the steering column switch unit assembly in place and attach the tab to the steering column retainer.
- Tighten the steering column switch unit retaining band.
- Connect the front and rear electrical connections for the steering column switch unit.
Op. 4110A10 STEERING WHEEL - R+R FOR REPLACEMENT OR CHECKS [→939010817]
Op. 5580C18 AIR BAG MODULE - R+R [→939011097]
Op. 7040A26 STEERING COLUMN TRIM - R+R [→939011231]

## 5550B30 L/H OR R/H HORN (ONE) - R+R  [939011048]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5550 WARNING AND MANOEUVRING INDICATORS > 5550B HORNS > 5550B30 L/H OR R/H HORN (ONE) - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011048

##### Removing
Op. 7055B54 UNDER ENGINE PROTECTION/GUARD - R.R. [→939011369]
1. Undo the nut.
2. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| SINGLE-TONE HORN | See P005 SINGLE-TONE HORN [→939004549] |
3. Remove the horns with the bracket.
4. If necessary, undo nuts (4a) and remove horns (4b) and (4c) from the bracket.

##### Refitting
- Check that the horns are not damaged and, if necessary, conduct an electrical test.

- Connect the electrical connection.
- Tighten the nut.

## 5550D10 BRAKE LIGHT SWITCH - R+R  [939011051]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5550 WARNING AND MANOEUVRING INDICATORS > 5550D BRAKE AND REVERSING LIGHTS > 5550D10 BRAKE LIGHT SWITCH - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011051

##### Removing
1. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| BRAKE PEDAL SWITCH | See I030 BRAKE PEDAL SWITCH [→939004116] |
2. Rotate the switch on the brake pedal (2a) through about 90° in an anti-clockwise and extract it from the mounting (2b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001770.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the switch is not damaged.
- Gently lower the brake pedal and fit the switch in place, then rotate it through about 90° in a clockwise direction until it is locked.
- Check that the brake lights are working.

## 5550D16 THIRD BRAKE LIGHT - R+R  [939011052]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5550 WARNING AND MANOEUVRING INDICATORS > 5550D BRAKE AND REVERSING LIGHTS > 5550D16 THIRD BRAKE LIGHT - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011052

##### Removing
1. Move the third brake light (1a) backwards and release the retainers (1b), then lift it up releasing the retainers (1c).
2. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| ADDITIONAL REAR LIGHT (THIRD BRAKE LIGHT) | See F040 ADDITIONAL TAIL-LIGHT ('THIRD BRAKE LIGHT') [→939004013] |
3. Keep the third brake light and moulding.
4. If necessary, undo the bolts (4a) and separate the third brake light (4b) from the moulding (4c).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000794.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the third brake light is not damaged.
- If removed, place the light on the moulding and secure it using the bolts.
- Connect the electrical connection.
- Place the third brake light in position and attach the retainers.
- Check the operation of the third brake light.

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Station Wagon
##### Removing
Op. 7040G36 BULB HOLDER FOR LOAD CARRYING COMPARTMENT lighting on tailgate - R.R. [→939005460]
Op. 7015A05 TAILGATE LOWER INTERIOR PANEL-R R [→939005348]
Op. 7015A06 TAILGATE UPPER INTERIOR PANEL - R.R. [→939005349]
Op. 7210G76 REAR SPOILER - R.R. [→939005555]
1. Working at the bench on the spoiler, undo the bolts fixing the third brake light.
2. Release the electrical half-connection from the retainer.
3. Remove the third brake light.
> Note: The light consists of LEDs that cannot be replaced and therefore cannot be opened.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005234.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Station Wagon
##### Refitting
- Check that the third brake light is not damaged.
- Position the third brake light.
- Attach the electrical half-connection to the retainer.
- Tighten the third brake light fixing bolts.
Op. 7210G76 REAR SPOILER - R.R. [→939005555]
Op. 7015A06 TAILGATE UPPER INTERIOR PANEL - R.R. [→939005349]
Op. 7015A05 TAILGATE LOWER INTERIOR PANEL-R R [→939005348]
Op. 7040G36 BULB HOLDER FOR LOAD CARRYING COMPARTMENT lighting on tailgate - R.R. [→939005460]
- Check the operation of the third brake light.

## 5550D22 REVERSING LIGHT SWITCH - R+R  [939011050]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5550 WARNING AND MANOEUVRING INDICATORS > 5550D BRAKE AND REVERSING LIGHTS > 5550D22 REVERSING LIGHT SWITCH - R+R

##### Removing
- Position the vehicle on a lift.
- Make sure that the ignition key is in the OFF position, then disconnect the battery terminal (-).
|  | Description | Connector |
| --- | --- | --- |
| - | Battery | See A001 BATTERY [→939004879] |
1. Disconnect the electrical connection from the reversing light switch.
|  | Description | Connector |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Reversing lights switch | See I020 REVERSING LIGHTS SWITCH [→939004114] |

- Connect the electrical connection to the reversing light switch.
- Connect the negative battery terminal.
- Remove the vehicle from the lift.

> Block applies to: engines: 3.2 JTS; all production periods

| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Reversing light switch | - | M | (Gearbox side) 1.7 |  |
- Connect the electrical connection for the reversing light switch.

## 5550D86 THIRD BRAKE LIGHT CABLE LOOM - R.R. OR REPLACE  [939005345]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5550 WARNING AND MANOEUVRING INDICATORS > 5550D BRAKE AND REVERSING LIGHTS > 5550D86 THIRD BRAKE LIGHT CABLE LOOM - R.R. OR REPLACE

##### Removing
Op. 7040G36 BULB HOLDER FOR LOAD CARRYING COMPARTMENT lighting on tailgate - R.R. [→939005460]
Op. 7015A05 TAILGATE LOWER INTERIOR PANEL-R R [→939005348]
Op. 7015A06 TAILGATE UPPER INTERIOR PANEL - R.R. [→939005349]
Op. 7210G76 REAR SPOILER - R.R. [→939005555]
- Raise the tailgate.
1. Disconnect the electrical connection for the third brake light wiring.

- Connect the electrical connection for the third brake light wiring.
- Lower the tailgate.
