## INTRODUCTION - ON BOARD INSTRUMENTS  [939011067]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5560 INSTRUMENTS > INTRODUCTION - ON BOARD INSTRUMENTS
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011067

#### WARNINGS
> Note: This vehicle is equipped with a starting system known as Smart-Key, where the traditional ignition key has been replaced with an electronic key, "T.E.G." (Transmitter for Entry and Go), combined with an electronic key reader and TEG-Reader radio frequency receiver.
The vehicle is not switched on/started up by inserting the key in the ignition switch (which is no longer present) and turning it, but by inserting the TEG into the TEG Reader and activating the START/STOP button.
Access to the vehicle through radiofrequency recognition of the remote control incorporated into the TEG is entirely the same as what takes place with the traditional remote control incorporated into the key fob.
The distribution of the power supply to the vehicle (ignition-controlled INT, INT/A, AVV, etc.) is managed by the steering lock node and takes place in entirely the same way as for models equipped with a traditional key.
The following documentation often still uses the expressions Key ON, Key OFF, ignition-operated power supply (INT) or key to MAR, key to STOP, key to AVV. These must be interpreted in accordance with the following summary table:
| Key ON | TEG inserted in TEG Reader |
| --- | --- |
| Key OFF | TEG removed from TEG Reader |
| ignition controlled power supply (INT) | TEG inserted into TEG Reader |
| key on MAR | TEG inserted in TEG Reader |
| key on STOP | TEG removed from TEG Reader |
| key on AVV | TEG inserted and START/STOP button pressed |
To prevent serious damage to the electrical and electronic components, never try and start the engine with the battery earth lead slack.
Never pull the leads or wiring using force because this would result in the cables being disconnected from the connectors or terminals.
Disconnect all the control units and electronic devices when carrying out arc welding operations on the bodyshell.
Before replacing a fuse, check that the circuit protected is not faulty.
When replacing fuses, make sure that the new fuse is the correct amperage. Do not use fuses that have a higher or lower amperage than required.

#### PRECAUTIONS
When working on electrical components, always follow very carefully the precautions described below:
- Remove rings, wrist watches and other metal objects.
- Take care with electrical arcs: Even if the voltage is 12V, the battery can produce high currents if there is a short circuit, creating arcs or sparks that can be dangerous to the mechanic or create a fire hazard;
- Take care over high voltages. The electronic ignition system produces voltages of 20,000V and more, which could be very dangerous. Take great care when working on or near these components.
- Prevent fire risks. Do not smoke when working close to the battery or components which get hot during usage (e.g. halogen bulbs) or in the area of the engine compartment when the engine is still hot.
Before working on the electrical system (connectors, electrical components, wiring etc.), always DISCONNECT THE BATTERY.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected, the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will therefore remain released and free to turn.

Op. 7040A26 STEERING COLUMN TRIM - R+R [→939011231]
- Place the steering wheel in the lower and retracted end of travel positions
1. Remove the trim (1a), releasing the retaining clips (1b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000808.jpg]

1. Undo the bolts.
2. Move the instrument panel slightly.
3. Disconnect the electrical connections (3a-b, 3c-d).
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| INSTRUMENT PANEL | See E050 INSTRUMENT PANEL [→939003981] |
4. Keep the control panel.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000809.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the control panel is not damaged.
- Connect the electrical connections.
- Position the trim and attach the retaining clips.
Op. 7040A26 STEERING COLUMN TRIM - R+R [→939011231]
> Note: Several of the CAN nodes, including the instrument panel, are programmed with default settings that the customer will find when they acquire the vehicle. If the panel is replaced, the Parts Dept. will send a "pristine" component. once fitted, the data memorized in the Body Computer must be transferred to it by carrying out the "PROXI ALIGNMENT" procedure using the Examiner.
- Check that the control panel instruments are working.

> Note: This vehicle is equipped with a starting system known as Smart-Key, whereby the traditional ignition key has been replaced with an electronic key "T.E.G.", (Transmitter for Entry and Go), combined with an electronic key reader and TEG-Reader radio frequency receiver.
Access to the vehicle through radiofrequency recognition of the remote control incorporated into the TEG is entirely the same as what takes place with the traditional remote control incorporated into the key fob.
The vehicle is not switched on/started up by inserting the key in the ignition switch (which is no longer present) and turning it, but rather by inserting the TEG in the TEG Reader and activating the START/STOP button.
The distribution of the power supply to the vehicle (ignition-controlled INT, INT/A, AVV, etc.) is managed by the steering lock node and takes place in entirely the same way as for models equipped with a traditional key.
The following documentation often still uses the expressions Key ON, Key OFF, ignition-operated power supply (INT) or key to MAR, key to STOP, key to AVV. These must be interpreted in accordance with the following summary table:
| Key ON | TEG inserted in TEG Reader |
| --- | --- |
| Key OFF | TEG removed from TEG Reader |
| ignition controlled power supply (INT) | TEG inserted into TEG Reader |
| key on MAR | TEG inserted in TEG Reader |
| key on STOP | TEG removed from TEG Reader |
| key on AVV | TEG inserted and START/STOP button pressed |
To prevent serious damage to the electrical and electronic components, never try and start the engine with the battery earth lead slack.
Never pull the leads or wiring using force because this would result in the cables being disconnected from the connectors or terminals.
Disconnect all the control units and electronic devices when carrying out arc welding operations on the bodyshell.
Before replacing a fuse, check that the circuit protected is not faulty.
When replacing fuses, make sure that the new fuse is the correct amperage. Do not use fuses that have a higher or lower amperage than required.

#### PRECAUTIONS
When working on electrical components, always follow very carefully the precautions described below:
- Remove rings, wrist watches and other metal objects.
- Take care with electrical arcs: Even if the voltage is 12V, the battery can produce high currents if there is a short circuit, creating arcs or sparks that can be dangerous to the mechanic or create a fire hazard;
- Take care over high voltages. The electronic ignition system produces voltages of 20,000V and more, which could be very dangerous. Take great care when working on or near these components.
- Prevent fire risks. Do not smoke when working close to the battery or components which get hot during use (e.g. halogen bulbs), nor in the area of the engine compartment when the engine is still hot.
Before working on the electrical system (connectors, electrical components, wiring etc.), always DISCONNECT THE BATTERY.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected, the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will therefore remain released and free to turn.

2. Disconnect the electrical connection.

1. Working in the front courtesy light opening, undo the bolts fixing the roof lining.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000775.jpg]

1. Release the roof lining rear retaining buttons using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
2. Working in the rear courtesy light opening, undo the fixing nut, then support the roof lining.
3. Pull the roof lining (3a) forwards and release the rear retainers (3b) from the housings (3c) for the roof lining.
4. Lower the roof lining.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000776.jpg]

1. Disconnect the connection for the aerial lead.
2. Undo the special nut with the washer.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000777.jpg]

1. Working from outside the vehicle, remove the aerial mounting and bridge.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000778.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the aerial is not damaged.
- Fit the bridge, position and aerial mounting and secure it from the inside using the special nut.
- Connect the connection for the aerial lead.
- Attach the roof lining to the rear retainers and support it.
- Tighten the nut fixing the roof lining in the rear courtesy light housing.
- Attach the rear etaining buttons.
- Working in the front courtesy light opening, tighten the bolts fixing the roof lining.
- Raise the front seat backrests into the end of travel position.

1. Working in the front courtesy light opening, undo the bolts fixing the roof lining.

- Connect the front connection for the aerial lead.
- Attach the roof lining to the rear retainers and support it.
- Tighten the nut fixing the roof lining in the rear courtesy light housing.
- Attach the rear etaining buttons.
- Working in the front courtesy light opening, tighten the bolts fixing the roof lining.

## 5570T49 BLUETOOTH ELECTRONIC CONTROL UNIT - R.R.  [939004927]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5570 ACCESSORIES > 5570T CAR RADIO AND CAR TELEPHONE > 5570T49 BLUETOOTH ELECTRONIC CONTROL UNIT - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939004927

##### Removing
- Make sure that the battery is disconnected.
Op. 5570T80 CAR RADIO EQUIPMENT - R+R [→939011077]
1. Insert the tools (1a) fully in the housings (1b) in the control unit (1c).
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 46515517 | Extractor | Radio extraction |  |
2. Grip the tools (1a) and pushing them outwards and pulling them towards yourself, release the inner retainers for the control unit (1c), taking care not to damage the retainers (2b).
3. Carefully release the retainers (2b) and extract the control unit (1c); if necessary, extract the control unit attaching it to the openings (3b).
- Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| BLUETOOTH CONTROL UNIT | See M162 BLUETOOTH CONTROL UNIT [→939004392] |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/946001024.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the control unit is not damaged.
- Check the electrical connection.
- Place the control unit in position and attach the inner and outer retainers.
Op. 5570T80 CAR RADIO EQUIPMENT - R+R [→939011077]
> Note: The control unit must be connected electrically at least 5 minutes before the key on.
> Note: Some of the CAN nodes, including the Bluetooth control unit, are programmed with default settings that the customer will find when they purchase the vehicle. If the control unit is being replaced, the Parts Dept. will send a pristine component: once fitted, the data memorized in the Body Computer must be transferred to it by carrying out the PROXY ALIGNMENT procedure using the Examiner.
- Once a new control unit is installed it is necessary to:
update the software;
check and, if necessary, set the voice message language correctly;
carry out the pairing operation for the telephones to be connected.
See descriptions 5570 ACCESSORIES [→939003541 5570 ACCESSORIES]
- Test the operation of the systems involved.

## 5570T52 CENTRE SPEAKER IN DASHBOARD - R.R.  [939004808]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5570 ACCESSORIES > 5570T CAR RADIO AND CAR TELEPHONE > 5570T52 CENTRE SPEAKER IN DASHBOARD - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939004808

##### Removing
1. Working in the centre of the dashboard, place the air vents in the closed air flow position.
2. Undo the bolts.
3. Move the vent assembly (3a) slightly releasing the flexible retainers (3b).
4. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| CENTRAL FROMT SPEAKER | See P032 CENTRE FRONT SPEAKER [→939004575] |
5. Undo the bolts.
6. Remove the speaker.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004772.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the front speaker is not damaged.
- Remove the speaker.
- Tighten the bolts.
- Connect the electrical connection.
- Position the vent assembly and attach the retainers.
- Tighten the bolts.
- Check that the speaker is working.

## 5570T80 CAR RADIO EQUIPMENT - R+R  [939011077]

| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |

## INTRODUCTION - SPECIAL ACCESSORIES  [939011083]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5580 SPECIAL ACCESSORIES > INTRODUCTION - SPECIAL ACCESSORIES
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011083

#### WARNINGS
> Note: This vehicle is equipped with a starting system known as Smart-Key, whereby the traditional ignition key has been replaced with an electronic key "T.E.G.", (Transmitter for Entry and Go), combined with an electronic key reader and TEG-Reader radio frequency receiver.
The vehicle is not switched on/started up by inserting the key into the ignition switch (which is no longer present) and turning it, but rather by inserting the TEG into the TEG Reader and activating the START/STOP button.
Access to the vehicle through radiofrequency recognition of the remote control incorporated into the TEG is entirely the same as what takes place with the traditional remote control incorporated into the key fob.
The distribution of the power supply to the vehicle (ignition-controlled INT, INT/A, AVV, etc.) is managed by the steering lock node and takes place in entirely the same way as for models equipped with a traditional key.
The following documentation often still uses the expressions Key ON, Key OFF, ignition-operated power supply (INT) or key to MAR, key to STOP, key to AVV. These must be interpreted in accordance with the following summary table:
| Key ON | TEG inserted in TEG Reader |
| --- | --- |
| Key OFF | TEG removed from TEG Reader |
| ignition controlled power supply (INT) | TEG inserted into TEG Reader |
| key on MAR | TEG inserted in TEG Reader |
| key on STOP | TEG removed from TEG Reader |
| key on AVV | TEG inserted and START/STOP button pressed |
To prevent serious damage to the electrical and electronic components, never try and start the engine with the battery earth lead slack.
Never pull the leads or wiring using force because this would result in the cables being disconnected from the connectors or terminals.
Disconnect all the control units and electronic devices when carrying out arc welding operations on the bodyshell.
Before replacing a fuse, check that the circuit protected is not faulty.
When replacing fuses, make sure that the new fuse is the correct amperage. Do not use fuses that have a higher or lower amperage than required.

#### PRECAUTIONS
When working on electrical components, always follow very carefully the precautions described below:
- Remove rings, wrist watches and other metal objects.
- Take care with electrical arcs: Even if the voltage is 12V, the battery can produce high currents if there is a short circuit, creating arcs or sparks that can be dangerous to the mechanic or create a fire hazard;
- Take care over high voltages. The electronic ignition system produces voltages of 20,000V and more, which could be very dangerous. Take great care when working on or near these components.
- Prevent fire risks. Do not smoke when working close to the battery or components which get hot during use (e.g. halogen bulbs), nor in the area of the engine compartment when the engine is still hot.
Before working on the electrical system (connectors, electrical components, wiring, etc.) turn the ignition key OFF and DISCONNECT THE BATTERY.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected, the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will therefore remain released and free to turn.

## INTRODUCTION - AIR BAG SYSTEM  [939011100]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5580 SPECIAL ACCESSORIES > 5580C AIR BAG SYSTEM > INTRODUCTION - AIR BAG SYSTEM
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011100

#### SAFETY INSTRUCTIONS TO BE FOLLOWED FOR OPERATIONS ON VEHICLES FITTED WITH AIR BAG SYSTEMS
> Note: The following instructions MUST BE FOLLOWED during any operation involving vehicles equipped with Air Bags and pretensioners.

##### Preliminary instructions
> Note: It should be remembered that Air Bag modules should be handled with care. Their use, transportation and storage are regulated by the procedures for handling such components, which are given below.
Before carrying out any repair operations to the bodywork, welding or operations that involve removing the Air Bag modules or the control unit:
- Always disconnect the battery, i.e.: disconnect the two leads from the pole and insulate them taping them carefully.
- Disconnect the electrical connection for the control unit waiting at least 10 minutes after disconnecting the battery.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will therefore remain released and free to turn.
Position the devices with the cover upwards in the lockable metal cupboard. This cupboard, especially designed for this purpose, should not, under any circumstances, be used to store any other types of material, particularly if they are flammable. The cupboard should be designed to store pyrotechnic charges (metal, impact resistant cupboard with grates to allow natural ventilation) and should have the markings required by law (DANGER EXPLOSIVES - NO NAKED FLAMES - NOT TO BE OPENED BY UNAUTHORIZED PERSONNEL).
All the connectors used and wired to the Air Bag modules and/or pretensioners contain a short circuit clip. Until the Air Bag modules and/or pretensioners are connected to an appropriate energy source there is no possibility of them being accidentally activated.
> Note: The continuity of the system components can only be measured after the modules have been replaced with specific dummy resistances.
A system component that has not been activated during an accident is considered to still be "active". Therefore unexploded components that have to be removed from the vehicle (on account of defects or the warranty has expired, etc.) should be returned to the special centre following the procedure described below.
> Note: The safety system components should ONLY be fitted and dismantled by skilled, authorized personnel. Failure to follow the procedures described below could result in the accidental activation of the system, personal injury or repairs to the system that are not necessary. AIR BAG MODULES AND/OR PRETENSIONERS MUST NOT, UNDER ANY CIRCUMSTANCES, BE DISMANTLED..
All the system components have been specifically designed to work on a specific marque and type of vehicle, therefore Air Bags should not be adapted, reused or installed on other vehicles, only the ones for which they have been designed and produced.
> Note: Any attempt to resue, adapt or install one on a different type of vehicle could result in serious injury or even be fatal for the occupants of the vehicle in the case of an accident.

##### Interventions on system components
After every repair to the system it should be checked using the EXAMINER or other diagnostic equipment.

###### Module duration
The system pyrotechnic components have different life spans.
Therefore they must be replaced at the following intervals:
- Air bag modules or pretensioners: 14 years
- Clock spring: 10 years

###### Air bag module replacement (due to defects or expiry of warranty)
If an Air Bag module is being replaced because it is defective or the warranty has expired:
- Remove the detachable part of the adhesive label from the new module and place it in the special folder containing the vehicle details (model, registration date, chassis number) and the serial number of the old module. The file should be kept for future checks.
- A new adhesive plate is supplied together with the module and it should be stuck on top of the one located either near the driver (door or centre pillar) or in the passenger glove compartment. However, it must be perforated first at the month 14 years after the year in which the module is fitted (e.g. 2019 for 2005).
Similarly, if the clock spring or a pretensioner is being replaced, remember that the recommended life of these components is 10 years.

###### Control unit replacement
The electronic control unit should ALWAYS be replaced after an impact that results in the entire system being activated (Air Bag and pretensioners).
If the control unit is being replaced, the detachable part of the adhesive label must also be removed and placed the special file mentioned above.

###### Replacing the clock spring
The clock spring for connecting the driver's Air Bag module is equipped with a device that automatically stops it rotating when the steering wheel is removed. This is designed to prevent the upper plate, now no longer secured to the steering wheel, from turning freely and unwinding or winding the coil inside the device and possibly causing it to break. The device is automatically released when refitting the steering wheel.
In the case of replacement, the replacement clock spring is supplied separately from the steering column switch unit and it should be fitted with the wheels straight because this is the corresponding position for the new device. The new device is equipped with a safety tab that keeps it locked.

> Block applies to: engines: 1.9 JTD 16V; all production periods
This tab should be removed when fitting the steering wheel to allow the system to rotate correctly.

##### Painting operations
There are no special safety regulations to follow for painting and subsequent drying operations in an oven because the modules in particular have been designed in such a way that the outside surfaces of the vehicle are not damaged when they get hot from normal paint drying systems.
It is forbidden to use naked flames near the modules.
All the electronic control units (including the Air Bag system) are always removed if the ambient temperature could reach or exceed 85°C.

##### Interventions after an accident
If any of the safety system components is damaged following an accident it MUST be replaced.
Do not attempt to repair the control unit, clock spring or Air Bag modules and/or pretensioners.

###### Accidents with or without system activation
Several of the system components should be inspected after an accident whether the system has been activated or whether it has NOT been activated. These components are:
- Steering column;
- Steering column supports;
- Electronic control unit and modules mounting area;
- Clock spring;
- Dashboard (the area of the passenger Air Bag module and knee bag).
If the component shows signs of distortion, breaks or bends it should be replaced.

###### Accidents with system activation
Several of the system components should be replaced if the vehicle is subject to a frontal impact that has fully or partly activated the system.
In the case of partial activation (pretensioners, Side Bags or Head Bags), these components are:
- Pretensioners, Side Bags or Head Bags
- Electronic control unit (only after the third activation of the pretensioners, Side Bags or Head Bags)
In the case of total activation (Air Bags, Side Bags, Head Bags and pretensioners), these components are:
- Front Air Bag modules
- Knee Bag modules
- Pretensioners, Side Bags or Head Bags
- Electronic control unit
As far as the wiring and connectors are concerned, they should be inspected for any signs of burning, melting of the outside insulation or damage from excessive heat.
Any signs of damage to the clock spring or in the area where the electronic control unit and Air Bag modules are mounted means that the damaged components should be replaced.

##### Dangers to health
The following precautions should be observed when handling activated Air Bag modules and/or pretensioners:
- wear polythene protective gloves and safety goggles;
- wash your hands and other parts of the body exposed with soap and water after touching exploded Air Bag units.

###### Effects of overexposure
There is no potential danger of exposure to the propellants as the system is completely sealed.
The propellant mixture is in a solid state therefore it is impossible to inhale it even if the gas generator cartridge is broken.
There is no danger to human health if the gas escapes.
Contact with the skin should be avoided and the propellant should not be swallowed.
In the case of:
- Contact with skin: wash immediately with soap and water.
- Contact with eyes: wash eyes immediately with running water for at least 15 minutes.
- Inhalation: take the affected person into the open air immediately.
- Swallowing: induce vomiting if the person is conscious.
A doctor should always be called in these situations.

#### SAFETY REGULATIONS WHEN HANDLING MODULES
In normal conditions the driver's and passenger Air Bags and/or pretensioners are activated by the electronic command during the impact. The gases that develop in these conditions are not toxic.
> Note: It is important that the personnel who carry out repairs to devices fitted on the vehicle follow the safety regulations listed below.
The personnel working on the devices should be trained and should observe the following precautions:
- When removing and replacing open (exploded) Air Bags, only move the modules one at a time and wear protective gloves and goggles for the removal.
Always rest the Air Bag module with the opening flap and the pre-breaking splining facing upwards. Never place anything on top of this flap.
- Do not hold pretensioners by the rod when handling.
- At the end of the operations wash your hands thoroughly with soap and, if there is any contact with the residual powder and your eyes, rinse immediately with plenty of running water.
- On all versions with Air Bags it is forbidden to work from the front seats before having rendered the system inoperative by disconnecting the two battery leads and waiting at least 10 minutes.
- The metal Air Bag components are very hot just after it has exploded. Avoid touching these components for 20 minutes after the Air Bag has been activated.
- Never carry out repair operations on Air Bag modules or pretensioners. Send all defective modules to the supplier.
- Never heat the Air Bag module (e.g. through welding, striking, drilling, mechanical machining, etc.);
- Never fit Air Bag units and/or pretensioners that have been dropped or show signs of any type of damage.
> Note: The storage of Air Bag modules together with flammable or combustible materials is prohibited.
> Note: Gas generators should not come into contact with acid, grease or heavy metal: contact with these substances could cause the formation of gases that are poisonous or harmful or explosive compounds.
Replacement parts should be stored in their original packaging and the same procedures as for Air Bag modules that have not been activated and have been removed from the vehicle should be followed for their temporary storage, i.e. in a specially designed, locked, metal cupboard (impact resistant metal cupboard with grates for natural ventilation)
The cupboard should have special warning signs (DANGER: EXPLOSIVES - NO NAKED FLAMES - NOT TO BE OPENDED BY UNAUTHORIZED PERSONNEL).

#### SCRAPPING AIR BAG MODULES OR PRETENSIONERS
The Air Bag modules and/or pretensioners fitted on the vehicle should not be scrapped with the actual vehicle, but removed first.
The Air Bag units and/or pretensioners cannot be scrapped unless they have been activated first.
If the Air Bag module and/or pretensioners has not been activated during an accident it should be considered as still charged.
All unexploded material SHOULD NOT BE ACTIVATED, but should be sent to a specialist centre with the following written on the accompanying docket: "AIR BAG CONTAINING PYROTECHNIC CHARGE TO BE DEACTIVATED".
The local laws in force must be observed.
The devices must be sent in the packaging that replacement components are received in and, if it is not available, the packaging only can be ordered from the PARTS DEPT.
Obviously, if Air Bags and/or pretensioners are being replaced, the original packaging should be kept intact for dispatching devices not activated.
> Note: Not following the procedures described here can cause the accidental activation of Air Bag units and/or pretensioners and result in personal injury.
> Note: Air Bag units that have NOT been activated should be disposed of through normal methods.Air Bag units and/or pretensioners that have not bee activated contain substances that are dangerous to health and can cause personal injury if the sealed container is damaged during disposal.The disposal of Air Bag units and/or pretensioners not carried out in accordance with this procedure may be in violation of regulations in force on this subject.

#### ORDERING METHODS
If necessary, the devices can be ordered, as and when required, from the Aftersales Parts Department - Volvera through the V.O.R. procedure since the Network should not keep these parts in stock.
In any case, a record should be kept of internal movements by recording the module identification numbers and vehicle details (chassis number, registration date, model, etc.).

> Note: Do not strike or shake the control unit. Do not connect the electrial connections to the control unit before having correctly secured the control unit. Make sure that the battery is disconnected.
> Note: In the case of replacement, remove the detachable part of the adhesive label from the new module and place it in the special folder containing the vehicle details (model, registration date, chassis number) and the serial number of the old control unit. The file should be kept for future checks.
1. Position the control unit (1a). Make sure that the arrow (1b) on the top of the control unit is facing the direction of travel.
2. Tighten the fixing nuts to the recommended torque.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001754.jpg]

| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| AIR BAG CONTROL UNIT | Nut | M6 | 0.65 ÷ 0.95 |  |
- Connect the electrical connections.
Op. 5010B70 SIDE CONNECTOR BRACKETS ONE, LH OR RH, BETWEEN AIR CONDITIONER CASING AND DUCT FOR R [→939010890]
Op. 7040A65 CONSOLE SIDE CLOSING (ONE) - R.R. [→939011230]
- Use an Examiner to check that the system has not acquired errors during the operation.

## 5580C18 AIR BAG MODULE - R+R  [939011097]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5580 SPECIAL ACCESSORIES > 5580C AIR BAG SYSTEM > 5580C18 AIR BAG MODULE - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011097

##### Removing
> Note: Before removing and refitting the Air Bag module the following preliminary operations must be carried out.
- Place the steering wheel in the centre with the wheels straight. Ensure that the electronic key is inserted in the TEG Reader
- Disconnect the battery leads and insulate them.
- Wait for 10 minutes before continuing.
> Note: FOLLOW ALL THE RECOMMENDED SAFETY INSTRUCTIONSOp. 5580C AIR BAG SYSTEM [→939011086]
1. Undo the bolts.
2. Remove the lower steering column trim.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001767.jpg]

1. Turn the steering wheel to gain access to one of the two holes (1a) from side (1b).
2. Fit the appropriate tool fully into accessible hole (1b), then turn to release retainer (2a) of airbag (2b).
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 2000005800 | Extractor | Air Bag module extraction |  |
3. Turn the steering wheel to gain access to the other of the two holes (1a) from side (1b).
4. Fit the appropriate tool fully into accessible hole (1b), then turn to release retainer (2a) of airbag (2b).
5. Disconnect the electrical connections (5a, 5b).
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| DRIVER'S AIR BAG | See Q009 DRIVER'S AIR BAG [→939004894] |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001768.jpg]

- Keep the Air Bag module.
- Place the Air Bag module in the special cupboard resting it on a free surface with the bag facing upwards.

##### Refitting
- Observe the following precaution.
> Note: Handle the Air Bag module very carefully. Make sure that the battery is disconnected.
- If a new module is being fitted, position the labels. Remove the detachable part of the adhesive label from the new module and place it in the special folder containing the vehicle details (model, registration date, chassis number) and the serial number of the old module. The file should be kept for future checks. A new adhesive plate comes together with the module and should be stuck on top of the existing one. Beforehand, however, it should be perforated at the month and 14 years after the year in which the module is being fitted (e.g. 2005 for 2019).
- Position the Air Bag module connecting the electrical connections.
- Place the airbag module in position and attach the retainers.
- Position the lower steering column cover and tighten the fixing bolts.
- Connect the battery.
- Use an Examiner to check that the system has not acquired errors during the operation.

## 5580C26 PASSENGER AIR BAG DISABLING KEY LOCK BARREL/SWITCH - R.R.  [939011094]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5580 SPECIAL ACCESSORIES > 5580C AIR BAG SYSTEM > 5580C26 PASSENGER AIR BAG DISABLING KEY LOCK BARREL/SWITCH - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011094

##### Removing
> Note: Before removing and refitting the control unit the following preliminary operations must be carried out.
- Extract the electronic key.
- Disconnect the battery.
1. Extract the vent (1a), releasing the retaining clips (1b).
2. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| PASSENGER AIRBAG DISABLEMENT SWITCH | See H075 PASSENGER AIR BAG DISABLING SWITCH [→939004099] |
3. Keep the switch
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001764.jpg]

- If necessary, remove the airbag switch barrel as follows.
> Note: It is not possible to order key lock barrels on their own or to order lock barrels with the part code only. To order lock barrels, follow the special procedure illustratedOp. 5580E ANTI-THEFT DEVICE [→939011101]
1. Remove the lock retaining clip
2. fit the vehicle key in the barrel.
3. Remove the barrel.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001765.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the switch and the lock are not damaged and, if necessary, conduct an electrical test.
- Position the barrel in its housing.
- Fit the clip and do up.
- Remove the vehicle key from the barrel.
- Connect the electrical connection.
- Insert the switch and do up the retaining clips.
- Connect the battery.
- Use an Examiner to check that the system has not acquired errors during the operation.

Op. 7045A25 FRONT SEAT BACKREST LUMBAR ADJUSTMENT KNOB (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011290]
1. Lower the trim and padding (1a) and release the panel of lumbar adjustment knob (1b) from retainers (1c).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004131.jpg]

1. Disconnect the backrest trim links.
2. Release retainers (2a) from retainers (2b) along vertical sections (2c) and (2d).
3. Disconnect retainer (3a) from crossmember (3b).
4. Remove rear pane (4a) by releasing from upper retainers (4b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004132.jpg]

1. Release retainer (1a) of the upper edge of the backrest trim from metal profile (1b).
2. Press the disconnected edge in the upper part of the seat.
3. Disconnect retainer (3a) by releasing links (3b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004133.jpg]

1. Push the trim and padding slightly aside to gain access to the airbag module.
2. Cut the wiring retaining bands.
3. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| DRIVER'S SIDE BAG | See Q030 DRIVER'S SIDE BAG [→939004633] |
| PASSENGER SIDE BAG | See Q031 PASSENGER SIDE BAG [→939004636] |
4. Undo the nuts fixing the Air Bag module.
5. Disconnect the electrical earth connection.
6. Remove the Air Bag module.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004134.jpg]

- Place the Air Bag module in the special cupboard resting it on a free surface with the bag facing upwards.

##### Refitting
- Observe the following precaution.
> Note: Handle the Air Bag module very carefully. Make sure that the battery is disconnected.
- If a new module is being fitted, position the labels as described below.
- Remove the detachable part of the adhesive label from the new module. Place a copy of the label in a special folder containing the vehicle details (model, registration date, chassis number) and the serial number of the old module. The file should be kept for future checks.
Fit the detachable part of the original label on the lower part of the seat, where similar labels for the seat, the backrest cover and the Air Bag wiring can already be found. Beforehand, however, it should be perforated at the month and 14 years after the year in which the module is being fitted (e.g. 2005 for 2019).
- Remove the Side Bag module.
- Connect the electrical earth connection.
- Tighten the nuts fixing the Air Bag module.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| SIDE AIR BAG MODULE | Nut | M5 | 0.4 ÷ 0.6 |  |
- Connect the electrical connection for the Air Bag module.
- Fit the retaining bands on the wiring.
- Position the trim with padding in its housing.
- Tension the links and connect to the retainer.
- Position the front edge of the backrest trim in its seat and connect to the metal profile.
- Position the rear panel and do up the upper retainers.
- Connect the retainer to the crossmember.
- Connect the rear panel vertical retainers.
- Connect the backrest trim links.
- Attach the lumbar adjustment knob panel to the retainers.
Op. 7045A25 FRONT SEAT BACKREST LUMBAR ADJUSTMENT KNOB (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011290]
Op. 7045A10 FRONT SEAT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011288]
- Use an Examiner to check that the system has not acquired errors during the operation.

## 5580C52 WINDOW BAG MODULE (ONE) LH OR RH - R.R WITH INTERFERING PARTS REMOVED  [939011093]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5580 SPECIAL ACCESSORIES > 5580C AIR BAG SYSTEM > 5580C52 WINDOW BAG MODULE (ONE) LH OR RH - R.R WITH INTERFERING PARTS REMOVED
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011093

##### Removing
> Note: Before removing and refitting the Air Bag module the following preliminary operations must be carried out.
- Extract the electronic key.
- Disconnect the battery leads and insulate them.
- Wait for 10 minutes before continuing.
> Note: FOLLOW ALL THE RECOMMENDED SAFETY INSTRUCTIONSOp. 5580C AIR BAG SYSTEM [→939011086]
- Lower the front seat backrests into the end of travel position.
1. Working via the front courtesy light housing, undo the bolts fixing the roof lining.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001756.jpg]

1. Release the roof lining rear retaining buttons using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
2. Working via the rear courtesy light housing, undo the fixing nut, then support the roof lining.
3. Pull the roof lining (3a) forwards and release the rear retainers (3b) from the housings (3c) for the roof lining.
4. Lower the roof lining.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001757.jpg]

1. Working from the rear of the vehicle, disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| PASSENGER SIDE WINDOW BAG | See Q041 PASSENGER WINDOW BAG [→939004641] |
| DRIVER SIDE WINDOW BAG | See Q040 DRIVER'S HEAD BAG [→939004639] |
2. Undo the rear bolts securing the window bag module.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001758.jpg]

1. Working at the front of the vehicle, unscrew the window bag link retaining bolt.
2. Disconnect the rod (2a) from retainers (2b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001759.jpg]

1. Undo the central bolts securing the window bag module.
2. Undo the module retaining studs using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
3. Recover the window bag module assembly.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001760.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Observe the following precaution.
> Note: Handle the Air Bag module very carefully. Make sure that the battery is disconnected.
- If a new module is being fitted, position the labels as described below.
- Remove the detachable part of the adhesive label from the new module and place it in the special folder containing the vehicle details (model, registration date, chassis number) and the serial number of the old module. The file should be kept for future checks. A new adhesive plate comes together with the module and should be stuck on top of the existing one. Beforehand, however, it should be perforated at the month and 14 years after the year in which the module is being fitted (e.g. 2005 for 2019).
1. Position the window bag module.
2. Attach the blind retaining studs.
3. Tighten the blind retaining bolts.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| WINDOW BAG MODULE | Bolt | M6x20 | 0.65 ÷ 0.95 |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001761.jpg]

1. Connect pipe (1a) to retainers (1b).
2. Tighten the front rod retaining bolt
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| WINDOW BAG MODULE ROD | Bolt | M6x20 | 0.65 ÷ 0.95 |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001762.jpg]

1. Tighten the rear bolts securing the window bag module.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| SIDE AIR BAG MODULE | Nut | M5 | 0.4 ÷ 0.6 |  |
2. Connect the electrical connection.
- Attach the roof lining to the rear retainers and support it.
- Tighten the nut fixing the roof lining in the rear courtesy light housing.
- Attach the rear retaining buttons.
- Working in the front courtesy light opening, tighten the bolts fixing the roof lining.
- Raise the front seat backrests into the end of travel position.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001763.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Station Wagon

## 5580C86 SIDE BAG CABLE LOOM (ONE) LH OR RH - R.R OR REPLACE  [939011099]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5580 SPECIAL ACCESSORIES > 5580C AIR BAG SYSTEM > 5580C86 SIDE BAG CABLE LOOM (ONE) LH OR RH - R.R OR REPLACE
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011099

##### Removing
> Note: FOLLOW ALL THE RECOMMENDED SAFETY INSTRUCTIONSOp. 5580C AIR BAG SYSTEM [→939011086]
Op. 7045A10 FRONT SEAT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011288]
Op. 7045A25 FRONT SEAT BACKREST LUMBAR ADJUSTMENT KNOB (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011290]
Op. 5580C44 FRONT SIDE BAG MODULE (ONE) LH OR RH - R.R [→939011098]
1. Release the rear part (1a) of the cushion trim from retainers (1b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005473.jpg]

1. Disconnect the electrical connection for the seat heating control unit.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| PASSENGER FRONT SEAT HEATER CONTROL UNIT | See M110 FRONT PASSENGER SEAT HEATER CONTROL UNIT [→939004389] |
| DRIVER'S FRONT SEAT HEATER CONTROL UNIT | See M170 DRIVER'S FRONT SEAT HEATER CONTROL UNIT [→939004394] |
2. Undo retainers (2a) from seats (2b); release retainer (2c) from the frame and move the electronic thermostat with mount (2d).
3. Disconnect the electrical connection for the cushion heater pad.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| LEFT SEAT HEATER | See O040 LEFT SEAT HEATER [→939004542] |
| PASSENGER SEAT HEATER | See O041 PASSENGER SEAT HEATER [→939004545] |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005474.jpg]

1. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| LEFT SEAT HEATER | See O040 LEFT SEAT HEATER [→939004542] |
| PASSENGER SEAT HEATER | See O041 PASSENGER SEAT HEATER [→939004545] |
2. Disconnec retainers (2a) securing airbag wiring harness (2b) to the seat structure.
3. Disconnect retainer (3a) from backrest rotation pipe (3b).
4. Undo retainer (4a) and remove connector cover (4b) in the arrowed direction.
5. Disconnect the electrical connection for the side bag.
6. Recover the side bag wiring harness.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005475.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the wiring is intact and there are no signs of damage.
- If new wiring is being fitted, position the labels as described below.
- Remove the detachable part of the adhesive label from the new wiring. Place a copy of the label in a special folder containing the vehicle details (model, registration date, chassis number) and the serial number of the old backrest cover. The file should be kept for future checks.
- Fit the detachable part of the original label on the lower part of the seat, where similar labels for the seat,Side Bag module and backrest cover can already be found.
- Place the wiring on the frame and secure it using the retaining bands.
- Connect the electrical connection for the side bag module.
- Place the connector cover in position and attach the retainers.
- Connect the retainer to the backrest rotation pipe.
- Connect the airbag module wiring harness retainers.
> Note: To rule out any possibility of the wiring being compressed, lay along exactly the same route as the wiring removed.
- Connect the electrical connections.
- Position the seat heating control unit and do up the retainers.
- Connect the electrical connection for the seat heating control unit.
- Connect the rear part of the cushion trim to the retainers.
Op. 5580C44 FRONT SIDE BAG MODULE (ONE) LH OR RH - R.R [→939011098]
Op. 7045A25 FRONT SEAT BACKREST LUMBAR ADJUSTMENT KNOB (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011290]
Op. 7045A10 FRONT SEAT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011288]

1. Disconnect the electrical connections (1a, 1b, 1c, 1d, 1e).

| --- | --- |

| PASSENGER SEAT HEATER | See O041 PASSENGER SEAT HEATER [→939004545] |

| RIGHT FRONT SEAT FRONT HEIGHT ADJUSTMENT MOTOR | See N129 RIGHT FRONT SEAT FRONT HEIGHT ADJUSTMENT MOTOR [→939004504] |
| DRIVER'S SEAT BACKREST ADJUSTMENT MOTOR | See N132 DRIVER'S SEAT BACKREST ADJUSTMENT MOTOR [→939004508] |

2. Disconnec retainers (2a) securing airbag wiring harness (2b) to the seat structure.
3. Disconnect retainer (3a) connecting airbag wiring harness (2b) to seat belt anchorage link wiring harness (3b).
4. Disconnect retainer (4a) from backrest rotation pipe (4b).
5. Undo retainer (5a) and remove connector cover (5b) in the arrowed direction.
6. Disconnect the electrical connection for the side bag.
7. Recover the side bag wiring harness.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005526.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Electric seats
##### Refitting
- Check that the wiring is intact and there are no signs of damage.
- If new wiring is being fitted, position the labels as described below.
- Remove the detachable part of the adhesive label from the new wiring. Place a copy of the label in a special folder containing the vehicle details (model, registration date, chassis number) and the serial number of the old backrest cover. The file should be kept for future checks.
- Fit the detachable part of the original label on the lower part of the seat, where similar labels for the seat,Side Bag module and backrest cover can already be found.
- Place the wiring on the frame and secure it using the retaining bands.
- Connect the electrical connection for the side bag module.
- Place the connector cover in position and attach the retainers.
- Connect the retainer to the backrest rotation pipe.
- Connect the airbag wiring harness to the seat belt anchorage link wiring harness using the retainers.
- Connect the airbag module wiring harness retainers to the seat structure.
> Note: To rule out any possibility of the wiring being compressed, lay along exactly the same route as the wiring removed.
- Connect the electrical connections.
- Position the seat heating control unit and do up the retainers.
- Connect the electrical connection for the seat heating control unit.
- Connect the rear part of the cushion trim to the retainers.
Op. 5580C44 FRONT SIDE BAG MODULE (ONE) LH OR RH - R.R [→939011098]
Op. 7045A25 FRONT SEAT BACKREST LUMBAR ADJUSTMENT KNOB (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011290]
Op. 7045A10 FRONT SEAT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011288]
- Use an Examiner to check that the system has not acquired errors during the operation.

## ANTITHEFT DEVICE - GENERAL REMARKS  [939011102]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5580 SPECIAL ACCESSORIES > 5580E ANTI-THEFT DEVICE > ANTITHEFT DEVICE - GENERAL REMARKS
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011102

#### PROGRAMMING THE ELECTRONIC KEY (TEG)
The electronic key is programmed at end of line together with the ALFA CODE.
During the vehicle's lifetime, it is possible to make the TEG Reader (NTR) learn new keys, e.g. if ELECTRONIC KEYS (TEGs) are added or deleted.
Upon each new programming procedure, all the Electronic Keys not presented during the above procedure (for the removal of lost keys) are cleared from the memory.
Upon each new programming procedure, all the Electronic Keys not presented during the above procedure (for the removal of lost keys) are cleared from the memory.

##### Procedure for storing an electronic key teg
The TEG storage procedure is managed by the Diagnostic instrument (Examiner) and can be activated only by entering the ELECTRONIC CODE (shown on the CODE CARD).
The procedure consists of learning the TEG codes and involves secret codes being received by the TEG Reader and then stored. During the procedure, the codes are stored permanently in the TEG Reader EEPROM memory.
Once the procedure is activated, the flow is as follows:
- the Diagnostic instrument commands the TEG Reader to initialise the TEG learning procedure and confirms this to the operator;
- the operator enters the first TEG to be stored;
- the TEG Reader receives the secret code and the ID of the ELECTRONIC KEY (TEG) and learns it;
- the Diagnostic instrument confirms that learning has taken place;
- the previous stages are repeated for each TEG that must be learned, up to a maximum of 8;
- once the TEGs have been learned, the operator asks the Diagnostic instrument to store them: the Diagnostic instrument commands the TEG Reader to store the TEGs learnt.
Only TEGs learned during the procedure are stored in the EEPROM. Any TEGs no longer presented will not be stored.

#### CODE SYSTEM COMPONENT MANAGMENT
There is a centralized DATA BASE (for all markets) managed by the FIAT PARTS DIVISION for ordering all CODE system components.
The production plant transfers all the necessary data into this data base together at the time of vehicle production.
FIAT PARTS then uses this data base for ordering components that need replacing by the Dealer Network.
The data base contains numerous pieces of information, such as:
- vehicle chassis no.;
- secret code;
- code of each ELECTRONIC KEY (TEG);
- emergency key mechanical code;
- Programming data
If one of the CODE system components needs replacing and is being ordered, follow the procedure described below.

##### Lost teg
Ask the Customer for all the remaining TEGs in their possession.
The Customer must prove that he or she is the owner of the vehicle by producing the registration document and identification data.
The codes of the lost TEGs must be cleared from the memory of the TEG Reader: proceed as follows:
- Connect the Tester to the tester output on the vehicle.
- Set the TEG Reader to PROGRAMMING mode.
- enter the TEGs still in the driver's possession into the TEG Reader.
- End PROGRAMMING mode; the IDs of any TEGs not presented will be cleared from the TEG Reader memory.
At the end of the procedure, print a table showing the identification code/s still enabled.
If no printer is available, transcribe the codes from the screen to the FIAT PARTS system form.
Only if the Body Computer already contains 8 ELECTRONIC KEYS (TEGs) send the codes to Fiat Parts to update the DATABASE.

##### Request for a new teg
Use the Personal Computer supplied to log onto Fiat Parts. Enter the chassis no. of the customer's car, the model code and set up a VOR order to request a new TEG (entering the part number) and specify the quantity.
Note that the Body Computer is designed to store up to 8 TEGs. It is not therefore possible to accept orders when the sum of the number of TEGs already available to the customer plus the new TEGs ordered exceed this quantity. Keep a record of the form used to order the TEGs in order to monitor the situation.

##### Programming teg codes into the teg reader memory
Once the new TEGs have been received from Fiat Parts, ensure that all the old TEGs in the customer's possession are available.
Connect the Tester to the vehicle tester output and run the following procedure.
- Set the TEG Reader to PROGRAMMING mode.
- enter the TEGs still in the driver's possession into the TEG Reader.
- end PROGRAMMING mode; the IDs of any TEGs not presented will be cleared from the TEG Reader memory.
- Check system operation before returning the vehicle to the customer by starting it up using all the TEGs and opening/closing the doors using the remote control unit.
> Note: In the case of operating faults, it is possible to order spare parts only, but it is absolutely forbidden to exchange electronic control units between different TEGs because it will not be possible to service the vehicle in the future.

##### Replacing lock barrels and/or emergency mechanical keys
This becomes necessary when one or more door lock barrels (driver's door lock or others) are forced.
The Customer must prove that he or she is the owner of the vehicle by producing the registration document and identification data.
Use the Personal Computer supplied to log onto Fiat Parts. Enter the chassis no. of the customer's car, the model code and set up a VOR order to order a single electronic key kit complete with dowels (entering the part number) for the vehicle type and specification.
> Note: It is not possible to order a single barrel by giving a single mechanical code.
> Note: The mechanical code of the emergency key and the electronic code of the TEG are defined together during initial memorisation on a vehicle at end of line and must not be separated under any circumstances.

##### Replacing the reg reader
Ask the Customer for all the remaining TEGs in their possession.
They should also prove that they are the owner of the vehicle by producing the registration document and identification data.
Use the Personal Computer supplied to log onto Fiat Parts. Enter the chassis no. of the customer's car, the model code and set up a VOR order to order a single TEG Reader (entering the part number to identify the version).
Once the new programmed TEG Reader has been received from Fiat Parts, proceed as follows:
- Check the serial number stamped on the plate applied to the order form corresponds to that shown on the TEG Reader.
- Check the vehicle chassis no. printed on the plate applied to the order form corresponds to the vehice chassis no.
- If all codes correspond, install the TEG Reader on the vehicle instead of the malfunctioning unit.
- Connect the Tester to the tester output on the vehicle.
- Set the TEG Reader to PROGRAMMING mode.
- enter the TEGs still in the driver's possession into the TEG Reader.
- End PROGRAMMING mode; the IDs of any TEGs not presented will be cleared from the TEG Reader memory (NTR).
- Check system operation before returning the vehicle to the customer by starting it up using all the TEGs and opening/closing the doors using the remote control unit.

##### Replacing the engine management control unit
This is required if the engine malfunctions.
The Customer must prove that he or she is the owner of the vehicle by producing the registration document and identification data.
Use the personal computer provided to log onto Fiat Parts. Enter the part number of the Engine Control Unit to be replaced.
Once the Engine Control Unit has been received from Fiat Parts, install it on the vehicle and insert the TEG in the TEG Reader to allow self-programming.
Press the START/STOP button with the clutch or brake depressed to start the vehicle and check the engine is operating correctly.

##### Replacing the electric steering lock
This is required if the electric steering lock malfunctions.
The Customer must prove that he or she is the owner of the vehicle by producing the registration document and identification data.
Use the personal computer provided to log onto Fiat Parts. Enter the part number of the Electric Steering Lock to be replaced.
Once the Electric Steering Lock has been received from Fiat Parts, install it on the vehicle and insert the TEG in the TEG Reader to allow self-programming.
After 10s, remove the TEG from the TEG Reader and wait for the steering to lock mechanically (this will take place after approx 5s).
Check that the steering is locked.

##### Replacing the body computer
This is required if the Body Computer malfunctions.
The Customer must prove that he or she is the owner of the vehicle by producing the registration document and identification data.
Use the Personal Computer supplied to log onto Fiat Parts. Enter the chassis no. of the customer's car, the model code and set up a VOR order to order a single Body Computer (entering the part number to identify the version).
Once the Body Computer has been received from Fiat Parts, install it on the vehicle and insert the TEG in the TEG Reader to allow alignment of the Secret Codes.
Press the START/STOP button with the clutch or brake depressed to start the vehicle and check the vehicle electrics are operating correctly.

## 5580E17 STEERING LOCK - R.R  [939011103]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5580 SPECIAL ACCESSORIES > 5580E ANTI-THEFT DEVICE > 5580E17 STEERING LOCK - R.R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011103

##### Removing
- With the electronic key (TEG) inserted, disconect the battery. In this way, the steering lock remains disengaged.
1. Undo the bolts.
2. Remove the lower steering column trim.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004136.jpg]

1. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| STEERING LOCK CONTROL UNIT (NBS) | See M089 STEERING LOCK CONTROL UNIT (NBS) [→939004386] |
2. Remove the shear bolts using a drift and mallet.
3. Remove steering lock device (3a) releasing it from brackets (3b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004137.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the steering lock device is not damaged.
- Position the steering lock device in its seat.
- Screw in and tighten the new special bolts until the heads shear off.
- Connect the electrical connection.
- Position the lower steering column cover and tighten the fixing bolts.
> Note: Several of the CAN nodes, including the instrument panel, are programmed with default settings that customers will find when they acquire the vehicle. If the panel is replaced, the Parts Dept. will send a blank component. once fitted, the data memorized in the Body Computer must be transferred to it by carrying out the PROXY ALIGNMENT procedure using the Examiner.
- Check that the steering lock is working.

## 5580E40 ANTI-THEFT DEVICE SIREN - R+R  [939004928]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5580 SPECIAL ACCESSORIES > 5580E ANTI-THEFT DEVICE > 5580E40 ANTI-THEFT DEVICE SIREN - R+R

##### Removing
- Open the bonnet.
1. Working behind the left front headlamp, disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| ALARM SIREN | See P090 ALARM SIREN [→939004618] |
2. Undo the bolts (2a) fixing the siren support (2b).
3. Remove the siren and support.
4. If necessary, undo the nuts (4a) and separate the siren (4b) from the support (2b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/946000666.jpg]

##### Refitting

- Tighten the bolts.
- Connect the electrical connection.
- Close the bonnet.

## 5580E50 ANTITHEFT DEVICE VOLUMETRIC SENSOR R R FOLLOWING ELECTRICAL CIRCUIT CHECK  [939011105]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5580 SPECIAL ACCESSORIES > 5580E ANTI-THEFT DEVICE > 5580E50 ANTITHEFT DEVICE VOLUMETRIC SENSOR R R FOLLOWING ELECTRICAL CIRCUIT CHECK
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011105

##### Removing
1. Move the front courtesy light assembly with volumetric sensor (1a), releasing the retainers (1b).
2. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| FRONT COURTESY LIGHT | See G010 FRONT COURTESY LIGHT [→939004020] |
3. Disconnect the volumetric sensor casing (3a) from the front courtesy light (3b) releasing the retainers (3c) and the attachments (3d).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001769.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the volumetric sensor is not damaged.
- Connect the volumetric sensor casing to the front courtesy light, engaging the attachments and the retainers.
- Connect the electrical connection.
- Position the front courtesy light assembly and volumetric sensor and attach the retainers.
- Check that the dismantled parts are working properly.

## 5580P CONNECT PANEL  [939004930]

## 5580P12 CONNECT PANEL REPLACE  [939004931]
- Path: Procedures > 55 ELECTRICAL EQUIPMENT > 5580 SPECIAL ACCESSORIES > 5580P CONNECT PANEL > 5580P12 CONNECT PANEL REPLACE
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939004931

##### Removing
1. Fit the tools (1a) in the housings (1b) in the navigator (1c), thenn tighten them until a click is heard (the release of the inner retainers (1d).
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1871003000 | Extractor | Extracting Connect panel |  |
2. Grip the tools (1a) and extract the navigator.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/946001025.jpg]

1. Disconnect the electrical connections (1a, 1b, 1c, 1d, 1e, 1f).
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| RADIO | See P020 RADIO [→939004551] |
2. Disconnect the aerial connection.
3. Keep the navigator and undo the tools from the housings.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/946001026.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the navigator is not damaged.
- Connect the aerial connection.
- Connect the electrical connections.
- Position the wiring carefully securing it to the retainers.
- Place the navigator carefully in position and attach the inner retainers. Take care not to pinch the electrical wires.
- Check that the navigator is working.

1. Undo the bolts.

1. Working in the luggage compartment, disconnect the electrical connection.

##### Refitting

- Position the subwoofer and attach the retainers.
- Tighten the bolts.
- Position the sound insulation.

- Applies to: all engines; all production periods

##### Removing
- Open the boot lid.
1. Working in the luggage compartment, disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
